    68	           '-m', str(v1.MODEL), '--port', '8091', '--ctx-size', '2048', '--threads', '4', '--threads-batch', '4',
    69	           '--parallel', '1', '--swa-full', '--cache-ram', '0', '--host', '127.0.0.1']
    70	    if cfg['mtp']:
    71	        cmd += v1.DRAFT_ARGS
    72	    if cfg['q8']:
    73	        cmd += ['--mmproj', str(v1.Q8), '--image-min-tokens', str(IMAGE_TOKENS), '--image-max-tokens', str(IMAGE_TOKENS)]
    74	    return cmd
    75	
    76	
    77	def files_of(cfg):
    78	    """Everything the server reads at load: weights it uses plus its build's libraries."""
    79	    return ([v1.MODEL] + ([v1.DRAFT] if cfg['mtp'] else []) + ([v1.Q8] if cfg['q8'] else [])
    80	            + [v1.BINS[BUILDS[cfg['build']]]] + libs_of(BUILDS[cfg['build']]))
    81	
    82	
    83	def libs_of(build):
    84	    return sorted(v1.BINS[build].parent.glob('lib*.so*'))
    85	
    86	
    87	def payloads(cfg):
    88	    """(warm-up, [(kind, name, endpoint, body)]) for the test; load tests send nothing."""
    89	    gen, unc, cached = v2.payloads()
    90	    first = ('F_' + gen[0][0], dict(gen[0][1], n_predict=FIRST_N))
    91	    test = cfg['test']
    92	    if test in LOAD_TESTS:
    93	        return None, []
    94	    if test == 'vision':
    95	        images = dict(v1.inputs()[1])
    96	        return (('image', WARMUP_FRAME, '/v1/chat/completions', images[WARMUP_FRAME]),
    97	                [('image', f, '/v1/chat/completions', images[f]) for f in VISION_FRAMES])
    98	    warm = ('first', *first[:1], '/completion', first[1])
    99	    if test == 'first_response':
   100	        return None, [warm]
   101	    if test == 'prompt':
   102	        seq = []
   103	        for k in range(ROUNDS):
   104	            seq += [('uncached', *unc), ('cached', *cached[k])]
   105	        return warm, [(kind, name, '/completion', body) for kind, name, body in seq]
   106	    return warm, [('gen', name, '/completion', body) for name, body in gen]
   107	
   108	
   109	def stamp_s(m):
   110	    mi, se, ms, us = map(int, m.groups())
   111	    return round(mi*60 + se + ms/1e3 + us/1e6, 3)
   112	
   113	
   114	def log_markers(text):
   115	    out = {}
   116	    for line in text.splitlines():
   117	        m = STAMP.match(line)
   118	        if m:
   119	            for key, needle in MARKERS.items():
   120	                if key not in out and needle in line:
   121	                    out[key] = stamp_s(m)
   122	    return out
   123	
   124	
   125	def freq_state(root=CPUFREQ):
   126	    """cpufreq time_in_state (10 ms ticks per frequency); readable without root."""
   127	    state = {}
   128	    for p in POLICIES:
   129	        rows = (line.split() for line in (root/p/'stats/time_in_state').read_text().splitlines() if line.strip())
   130	        state[p] = {int(f): int(t) for f, t in rows}
   131	    return state
   132	
   133	
   134	def freq_delta(a, b):
   135	    """Mean policy frequency (MHz) and share of time at its top frequency between two snapshots."""
   136	    out = {}
   137	    for p in POLICIES:
   138	        d = {f: b[p][f] - a[p].get(f, 0) for f in b[p]}
   139	        total = sum(d.values())
   140	        out[p] = dict(ticks=total, mean_mhz=round(sum(f*t for f, t in d.items())/total/1000) if total > 0 else None,
   141	                      top_share=round(d[max(d)]/total, 3) if total > 0 else None)
   142	    return out
   143	
   144	
   145	def mapped_libs(pid, build, maps=None):
   146	    """llama/ggml/mtmd libraries the running server actually mapped; all must come from its own build."""
   147	    text = maps if maps is not None else Path(f'/proc/{pid}/maps').read_text()
   148	    libs = sorted({m.group(1) for line in text.splitlines() if (m := LIB.search(line))})
   149	    want = str(v1.BINS[build].parent) + '/'
   150	    return dict(libs=libs, ok=bool(libs) and all(x.startswith(want) for x in libs))
   151	
   152	
   153	def verify(cfg):
   154	    """The running server must report the intended projector and MTP state and ctx 2048; never adjust flags."""
   155	    props, slots = v2.get('/props'), v2.get('/slots')
   156	    got = dict(vision=props.get('modalities', {}).get('vision'), speculative=[s.get('speculative') for s in slots],
   157	               n_ctx=[s.get('n_ctx') for s in slots], build_info=props.get('build_info'))
   158	    if got['vision'] is not cfg['q8'] or got['speculative'] != [cfg['mtp']] or got['n_ctx'] != [2048]:
   159	        raise RuntimeError(f"configuration mismatch: expected vision={cfg['q8']} mtp={cfg['mtp']} n_ctx=2048, "
   160	                           f'server reports {got}')
   161	    return got
   162	
   163	
   164	def snapshot(bridge):
   165	    """Operating conditions now: VIS-2 telemetry (with all its stops) plus cpufreq residency counters."""
   166	    d = v2.telemetry(bridge)
   167	    keep = ('skin', 'status', 'bat_c', 'capacity', 'voltage_now_uV', 'battery_w', 'mem_available_kib', 'max',
   168	            'cpuinfo_max_khz', 'cpu_mc', 't')
   169	    return dict({k: d.get(k) for k in keep}, freq=freq_state())
   170	
   171	
   172	def drop_caches(bridge):
   173	    """Same native cache-drop handshake as VIS-1/VIS-2."""
   174	    (bridge/'drop_done').unlink(missing_ok=True)
   175	    (bridge/'drop_request').touch()
   176	    began = time.monotonic()
   177	    while not (bridge/'drop_done').exists():
   178	        if time.monotonic()-began > 30:
   179	            raise RuntimeError('cache-drop handshake timeout')
   180	        time.sleep(.1)
   181	    if (bridge/'drop_done').read_text().strip() != 'ok':
   182	        raise RuntimeError('cache drop failed')
   183	    (bridge/'drop_request').unlink()
   184	    (bridge/'drop_done').unlink()
   185	
   186	
   187	def prime(paths):
   188	    """Read every file once so a non-cold test does not depend on what ran before (page cache warm)."""
   189	    began, n = time.monotonic(), 0
   190	    for p in paths:
   191	        with Path(p).open('rb') as f:
   192	            while chunk := f.read(8 << 20):
   193	                n += len(chunk)
   194	    return dict(bytes=n, s=round(time.monotonic()-began, 2))
   195	
   196	
   197	def launch(cmd, logpath, bridge, live):
   198	    """Spawn the owned server and wait for /health; load_s = spawn to /health ok (server readiness)."""
   199	    v1.free_ports()
   200	    with logpath.open('w') as log:
   201	        began = time.monotonic()
   202	        live['proc'] = proc = v1.spawn(cmd, log)
   203	    while live.get('pid') is None:
   204	        if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic()-began > 10:
   205	            raise RuntimeError('server crashed or owned child not found')
   206	        try:
   207	            live['pid'] = v1.server_pid(proc)
   208	        except (OSError, RuntimeError):
   209	            time.sleep(.05)
   210	    v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=live['pid']))
   211	    while True:
   212	        if proc.poll() is not None:
   213	            raise RuntimeError('server crashed during load')
   214	        try:
   215	            if v1.post('/health', timeout=1).get('status') == 'ok':
   216	                break
   217	        except (OSError, ValueError):
   218	            pass
   219	        v2.telemetry(bridge)
   220	        if time.monotonic()-began >= LOAD_TIMEOUT_S:
   221	            raise RuntimeError('server load timeout')
   222	        time.sleep(.05)
   223	    return time.monotonic()-began
   224	
   225	
   226	def stop(live, bridge, record):
   227	    v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=None))
   228	    proc, pid = live.pop('proc', None), live.pop('pid', None)
   229	    if proc is not None:
   230	        began = time.monotonic()
   231	        record['server_exit'] = v1.own_stop(proc, pid)
   232	        record['stop_s'] = time.monotonic()-began
   233	        record['exit_clean'] = record['server_exit'] == 0 and not v1.group_alive(proc.pid)
   234	
   235	
   236	def timed(bridge, pid, logpath, kind, name, endpoint, body, timeout):
   237	    offset = logpath.stat().st_size
   238	    f0, t = freq_state(), time.monotonic()
   239	    data = v2.guarded_post(bridge, pid, endpoint, body, timeout)
   240	    end, f1 = time.monotonic(), freq_state()
   241	    with logpath.open() as f:
   242	        f.seek(offset)
   243	        delta = f.read()
   244	    row = v1.request_record(name, data, t, end, delta, 'V2' if kind == 'image' else 'T2')
   245	    row.update(kind=kind, cache_n=row['timings'].get('cache_n'),
   246	               prompt_per_second=row['timings'].get('prompt_per_second'), freq=freq_delta(f0, f1))
   247	    return row
   248	
   249	
   250	def run_test(cfg, out, bridge):
   251	    record = dict(cfg, phase_start=time.monotonic(), label='NOT VALID', reason='interrupted', requests=[],
   252	                  command=command(cfg), started_epoch=time.time())
   253	    path, logpath = out/'result.json', out/'server.log'
   254	    live = {}
   255	    warm, seq = payloads(cfg)
   256	    try:
   257	        if v1.agent_found(log=True):
   258	            raise RuntimeError('agent found before launch')
   259	        if cfg['test'] == 'cold_load':
   260	            drop_caches(bridge)
   261	        else:
   262	            record['primed'] = prime(files_of(cfg))
   263	        record['start'] = snapshot(bridge)
   264	        if cfg['test'] in LOAD_TESTS:
   265	            record['measure_start'] = time.monotonic()
   266	        record['load_s'] = launch(record['command'], logpath, bridge, live)
   267	        if cfg['test'] in LOAD_TESTS:
   268	            record['measure_end'] = time.monotonic()
   269	            record['end'] = snapshot(bridge)
   270	        pid = live['pid']
   271	        v1.check_cores(pid)
   272	        record['reported'] = verify(cfg)
   273	        record['libs'] = mapped_libs(pid, BUILDS[cfg['build']])
   274	        if not record['libs']['ok']:
   275	            raise RuntimeError(f"server mapped libraries outside its build: {record['libs']['libs']}")
   276	        record['pss_after_load'] = v2.fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
   277	        if warm:  # discarded; first_response has none, its single request is the measurement
   278	            v2.guarded_post(bridge, pid, warm[2], warm[3], REQ_TIMEOUT_S)
   279	        if seq:
   280	            record['measure_start'] = start = time.monotonic()
   281	            record['measure_start_snapshot'] = snapshot(bridge)
   282	            for kind, name, endpoint, body in seq:
   283	                v2.telemetry(bridge)
   284	                v1.check_cores(pid)
   285	                left = TEST_CAP_S - (time.monotonic()-start)
   286	                if left <= 0:
   287	                    raise RuntimeError(f'test cap {TEST_CAP_S} s exceeded')
   288	                record['requests'].append(timed(bridge, pid, logpath, kind, name, endpoint, body, min(REQ_TIMEOUT_S, left)))
   289	                v1.write(path, record)
   290	            record['measure_end'] = time.monotonic()
   291	            record['end'] = snapshot(bridge)
   292	        record['freq'] = freq_delta((record.get('measure_start_snapshot') or record['start'])['freq'], record['end']['freq'])
   293	        record['pss_end'] = v2.fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
   294	        if live['proc'].poll() is not None:
   295	            raise RuntimeError('server crashed during the test')
   296	        v1.check_cores(pid)
   297	        record['stop_start'] = time.monotonic()  # monitor errors from the server's own exit come after this
   298	        stop(live, bridge, record)
   299	        if not record['exit_clean']:
   300	            raise RuntimeError(f"server cleanup not clean (exit {record['server_exit']})")
   301	        record.update(label='VALID', reason='completed ' + cfg['test'])
   302	    except (Exception, SystemExit) as e:
   303	        record.update(label='NOT VALID', reason=str(e))
   304	    finally:
   305	        record.setdefault('stop_start', time.monotonic())
   306	        stop(live, bridge, record)
   307	        record['stopped'] = time.monotonic()
   308	        if logpath.exists():
   309	            record['markers'] = log_markers(logpath.read_text(errors='replace'))
   310	        record['samples'] = [s for s in v1.rows(bridge/'samples.jsonl')
   311	                             if record['phase_start']-1 <= s['t'] <= record['stopped']]
   312	        record['lmk'] = v1.lmk_request(bridge, record['started_epoch'])
   313	        record['metrics'] = v1.metrics(record)
   314	        if record['label'] == 'VALID':
   315	            bad = [] if record['lmk'].get('ok') else ['LMK query failed']
   316	            if v2.sample_violations(record['samples']):
   317	                bad.append('sample violation: ' + ', '.join(v2.sample_violations(record['samples'])))
   318	            if [e for e in v1.rows(bridge/'errors.jsonl') if record['phase_start'] <= e['t'] <= record['stop_start']]:
   319	                bad.append('sampling failed')
   320	            if bad:
   321	                record.update(label='NOT VALID', reason='; '.join(bad))
   322	        v1.write(path, record)
   323	    return record
   324	
   325	
   326	def code_paths():
   327	    return ['benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py', 'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/oneshot.sh'] + v2.code_paths()
   328	
   329	
   330	def manifest(cfg):
   331	    warm, seq = payloads(cfg)
   332	    return dict(config=cfg, command=command(cfg),
   333	                payload_sha256={n: hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(b, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
   334	                                for _, n, _, b in ([warm] if warm else []) + seq},
   335	                code_sha256={p: v1.sha(REPO/p) for p in code_paths()},
   336	                weight_sha256={str(p): v1.sha(p) for p in WEIGHTS},
   337	                binary_sha256={str(p): v1.sha(p) for b in BUILDS.values() for p in [v1.BINS[b]] + libs_of(b)},
   338	                constants=dict(rounds=ROUNDS, first_n=FIRST_N, image_tokens=IMAGE_TOKENS, test_cap_s=TEST_CAP_S),
   339	                base_commit=subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=REPO, text=True).strip(),
   340	                git_status=subprocess.check_output(['git', 'status', '--porcelain=v1'], cwd=REPO, text=True))
   341	
   342	
   343	FINGERPRINT = ('config', 'command', 'payload_sha256', 'code_sha256', 'weight_sha256', 'binary_sha256', 'constants')
   344	
   345	
   346	def done_before(spec, runs=HERE/'runs'):
   347	    """A VALID result for the same test, configuration, code, payloads, weights and binaries already exists."""
   348	    want = {k: spec[k] for k in FINGERPRINT}
   349	    for result in sorted(runs.glob('*/result.json')):
   350	        try:
   351	            prior = json.loads((result.parent/'run.json').read_text())['manifest']
   352	            if json.loads(result.read_text()).get('label') == 'VALID' and {k: prior.get(k) for k in FINGERPRINT} == want:
   353	                return result.parent
   354	        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError):
   355	            continue
   356	    return None
   357	
   358	
   359	def check_env(env=os.environ):
   360	    """Both servers read LLAMA_ARG_* (batch, ubatch, flash attention, mmap, ...) and OpenMP variables from the
   361	    environment, which the command line would not show; refuse any so both run on their defaults."""
   362	    found = sorted(k for k in env if ENV_OVERRIDE.match(k))
   363	    if found:
   364	        raise SystemExit('environment overrides server settings; unset: ' + ', '.join(found))
   365	
   366	
   367	def preflight(cfg, repeat=False):
   368	    """Before launch, native and Debian: ports, agents, files/hashes, no shadowing libs, not already done,
   369	    plus Debian env/imports or native root/battery/cpuset."""
   370	    v1.free_ports()
   371	    matches = v1.agent_matches()
   372	    if matches:
   373	        v1.log_agent_matches(matches)
   374	        raise SystemExit('agent found; close Claude/AGY/Codex (see pid/comm/cmdline above)')
   375	    missing = [str(p) for p in files_of(cfg) if not p.is_file()]
   376	    if missing or not libs_of(BUILDS[cfg['build']]):
   377	        raise SystemExit('missing file(s): ' + ', '.join(missing or ['build shared libraries']))
   378	    # Both launchers list $PREFIX/lib before their own build dir in RUNPATH: a packaged libllama would shadow both.
   379	    shadow = sorted(p.name for p in (v1.NATIVE.parent/'usr/lib').glob('lib*') if re.match(r'lib(ggml|llama|mtmd)', p.name))
   380	    if shadow:
   381	        raise SystemExit('Termux $PREFIX/lib would shadow the build libraries: ' + ', '.join(shadow))
   382	    freq_state()
   383	    if Path('/termux-home').is_dir():  # Debian: the side that builds payloads and runs the servers
   384	        check_env()
   385	        needed = ['requests'] + (['PIL'] if cfg['test'] == 'vision' else [])
   386	        if [m for m in needed if importlib.util.find_spec(m) is None]:
   387	            raise SystemExit('Debian python3 is missing ' + ', '.join(needed) + ' — run: apt-get install python3-requests python3-pil')
   388	        spec = manifest(cfg)
   389	        bad = [p for p, h in WEIGHTS.items() if spec['weight_sha256'][str(p)] != h]
   390	        if bad:
   391	            raise SystemExit('SHA-256 mismatch: ' + ', '.join(map(str, bad)))
   392	        if not repeat and (prior := done_before(spec)):
   393	            raise SystemExit(f'already measured VALID with identical code and configuration: {prior} (--repeat to redo)')
   394	    else:
   395	        if subprocess.run(['su', '-c', 'id'], capture_output=True, timeout=10).returncode != 0:
   396	            raise SystemExit('no root (su) access; telemetry needs root')
   397	        cap, status = v1.root(f'cat {v1.BAT}/capacity {v1.BAT}/status', 'preflight_battery').split()[:2]
   398	        if not (cap.isdigit() and int(cap) >= 50 and status == 'Discharging'):
   399	            raise SystemExit('battery below 50% or not Discharging; unplug the charger')
   400	        before = os.sched_getaffinity(0)
   401	        os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
   402	        stuck = os.sched_getaffinity(0) == {4, 5, 6, 7}
   403	        os.sched_setaffinity(0, before)
   404	        if not stuck:
   405	            raise SystemExit('Termux cpuset blocks cores 4-7; bring Termux to the foreground, screen on')
   406	    print('PREFLIGHT OK', flush=True)
   407	
   408	
   409	def med(xs):
   410	    xs = [x for x in xs if x is not None]
   411	    return round(statistics.median(xs), 2) if xs else None
   412	
   413	
   414	def summary(folder):
   415	    """One result, from saved files only; missing fields are None (unavailable)."""
   416	    d = json.loads((Path(folder)/'result.json').read_text())
   417	    r, m = d['requests'], d.get('metrics', {})
   418	    by = lambda k: [x for x in r if x['kind'] == k]
   419	    dn = sum(x.get('draft_n') or 0 for x in r)
   420	    start = d.get('measure_start_snapshot') or d.get('start') or {}
   421	    return dict(
   422	        folder=str(folder), test=d['test'], build=d['build'], mtp=d['mtp'], q8=d['q8'], label=d['label'],
   423	        reason=d['reason'], load_s=med([d.get('load_s')]), markers=d.get('markers'),
   424	        first_ms=med([x['wall_ms'] for x in by('first')]),
   425	        uncached_pp_tok_s=med([x['prompt_per_second'] for x in by('uncached')]),
   426	        cached_ms=med([x['prompt_ms'] for x in by('cached')]),
   427	        gen_tok_s=med([x['predicted_per_second'] for x in by('gen')]),
   428	        draft_acceptance=round(sum(x.get('draft_n_accepted') or 0 for x in r)/dn, 3) if dn else None,
   429	        image_wall_ms=med([x['wall_ms'] for x in by('image')]),
   430	        image_tokens=sorted({x.get('image_tokens') for x in by('image')}, key=str),
   431	        prompt_n={x['input']: x['prompt_tokens'] for x in r}, gen_tokens={x['input']: x['generated_tokens'] for x in r},
   432	        checks=dict(uncached_cache_n_zero=all(x['cache_n'] == 0 for x in by('uncached')),
   433	                    cached_reuse=all((x['cache_n'] or 0) > 0 for x in by('cached')),
   434	                    replies_nonempty=all((x.get('reply') or '').strip() for x in r),
   435	                    libs_ok=(d.get('libs') or {}).get('ok')),
   436	        pss_after_load_mib=(d.get('pss_after_load') or {}).get('pss_kib') and round(d['pss_after_load']['pss_kib']/1024),
   437	        peak_pss_mib=m.get('peak_pss_kib') and round(m['peak_pss_kib']/1024),
   438	        min_mem_available_mib=m.get('min_mem_available_kib') and round(m['min_mem_available_kib']/1024),
   439	        mean_battery_w=(m.get('power') or {}).get('mean_battery_w'),
   440	        start=dict(skin=start.get('skin'), status=start.get('status'), bat_c=start.get('bat_c'),
   441	                   capacity=start.get('capacity'), max=start.get('max')),
   442	        skin_end=(d.get('end') or {}).get('skin'), status_max=m.get('status_max'),
   443	        caps={k: round(v['percent']) for k, v in m.get('caps', {}).items()},
   444	        freq=d.get('freq'), lmk=d.get('lmk'), exit_clean=d.get('exit_clean'))
   445	
   446	
   447	def conditions(a, b):
   448	    """Whether two results ran under matched operating conditions; each criterion reported separately."""
   449	    sa, sb = a['start'], b['start']
   450	    known = lambda *xs: all(x is not None for x in xs)
   451	    c = dict(
   452	        same_test=a['test'] == b['test'],
   453	        both_valid=a['label'] == b['label'] == 'VALID',
   454	        start_skin_within=known(sa['skin'], sb['skin']) and abs(sa['skin']-sb['skin']) <= MATCH_SKIN_C,
   455	        start_status_normal=sa['status'] == sb['status'] == 0,
   456	        battery_within=known(sa['capacity'], sb['capacity']) and abs(sa['capacity']-sb['capacity']) <= MATCH_BATTERY_PCT,
   457	        start_caps_equal=bool(sa['max'] and sb['max']) and all(sa['max'].get(p) == sb['max'].get(p) for p in POLICIES))
   458	    fa, fb = (x['freq'] or {} for x in (a, b))
   459	    mhz = {p: [(f.get(p) or {}).get('mean_mhz') for f in (fa, fb)] for p in POLICIES}
   460	    measured = all(known(*v) and abs(v[0]-v[1]) <= MATCH_FREQ_REL*max(v) for v in mhz.values())
   461	    return dict(c, matched=all(c.values()), start_skin=[sa['skin'], sb['skin']], measured_mean_mhz=mhz,
   462	                measured_freq_within=measured,
   463	                note='matched = start conditions only; measured frequency also reflects each build\'s own load')
   464	
   465	
   466	def report(*folders):
   467	    lines = [summary(f) for f in folders]
   468	    for line in lines:
   469	        print(json.dumps(line), flush=True)
   470	    if len(lines) == 2:
   471	        a, b = lines
   472	        num = lambda x: isinstance(x, (int, float)) and not isinstance(x, bool)
   473	        ratio = {k: round(b[k]/a[k], 3) for k in a if num(a[k]) and num(b[k]) and a[k]}
   474	        print(json.dumps(dict(second_over_first=ratio, conditions=conditions(a, b),
   475	                              prompt_n_identical=a['prompt_n'] == b['prompt_n'],
   476	                              gen_tokens_identical=a['gen_tokens'] == b['gen_tokens'])), flush=True)
   477	    return lines
   478	
   479	
   480	def parse(argv):
   481	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
   482	    ap.add_argument('--test', choices=TESTS)
   483	    ap.add_argument('--build', choices=tuple(BUILDS))
   484	    ap.add_argument('--mtp', action='store_true', help='load the MTP drafter (implied by --test mtp)')
   485	    ap.add_argument('--q8', action='store_true', help='load the Q8 projector, new build only (implied by --test vision)')
   486	    ap.add_argument('--repeat', action='store_true', help='measure again although an identical VALID result exists')
   487	    ap.add_argument('--dry-run', action='store_true')
   488	    ap.add_argument('--report', type=Path, nargs='+', help='print one result, or two plus their comparison')
   489	    ap.add_argument('--bridge', type=Path)
   490	    ap.add_argument('--preflight', action='store_true', help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
   491	    ap.add_argument('--monitor', type=Path, help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
   492	    return ap.parse_args(argv)
   493	
   494	
   495	def main(argv=None):
   496	    a = parse(argv)
   497	    if a.monitor:
   498	        return v1.monitor(a.monitor)
   499	    if a.report:
   500	        return report(*a.report)
   501	    if not (a.test and a.build):
   502	        raise SystemExit('--test and --build are required')
   503	    try:
   504	        cfg = config(a.test, a.build, a.mtp, a.q8)
   505	    except ValueError as e:
   506	        raise SystemExit(str(e))
   507	    if a.preflight:
   508	        return preflight(cfg, a.repeat)
   509	    if a.dry_run:
   510	        warm, seq = payloads(cfg)
   511	        print(DRY_LABEL)
   512	        print(json.dumps(dict(config=cfg, command=command(cfg), warmup=warm and warm[1],
   513	                              sequence=[(k, n, e) for k, n, e, _ in seq]), indent=1))
   514	        return
   515	    if a.bridge is None or not Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
   516	        raise SystemExit('launch only through native vis3/oneshot.sh into Debian')
   517	    out = bridge = a.bridge.resolve()
   518	    if not (bridge/'owner_launch').is_file():
   519	        raise SystemExit('missing owner wrapper receipt')
   520	    if v1.agent_found(log=True):
   521	        raise SystemExit('agent found; no server started')
   522	    check_env()
   523	    v1.free_ports()
   524	    v2.telemetry(bridge)
   525	    os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
   526	    v1.check_cores(os.getpid())
   527	    for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
   528	        signal.signal(sig, v1.interrupted)
   529	    spec = manifest(cfg)
   530	    if any(spec['weight_sha256'][str(p)] != h for p, h in WEIGHTS.items()):
   531	        raise SystemExit('model/drafter/projector SHA-256 mismatch')
   532	    if not a.repeat and (prior := done_before(spec)):
   533	        raise SystemExit(f'already measured VALID: {prior} (--repeat to redo)')
   534	    v1.write(out/'run.json', dict(manifest=spec, repeat=a.repeat,
   535	             note='One independent measurement; no thermal-equivalence gate. Compare with --report A B, '
   536	                  'which states whether start conditions matched.'))
   537	    print('RUN_FOLDER='+str(out), flush=True)
   538	    result = run_test(cfg, out, bridge)
   539	    print(f"{cfg['test']} {cfg['build']}{' mtp' if cfg['mtp'] else ''}{' q8' if cfg['q8'] else ''}: "
   540	          f"{result['label']} — {result['reason']}", flush=True)
   541	    report(out)
   542	    if result['label'] != 'VALID':
   543	        raise SystemExit(1)
   544	
   545	
   546	if __name__ == '__main__':
   547	    try:
   548	        main()
   549	    finally:
   550	        for proc in v1.OWNED:
   551	            v1.own_stop(proc)

     1	# VIS-3B: b1609-dotprod (old) vs b11716 (new), one independent test per launch, motors off
     2	
     3	Preparation only: no measured results yet. The owner runs it from native Termux with no agent resident. `vis3_run.py` imports
     4	`vis2/vis2_run.py` unchanged (and through it `vis1/vis1_run.py`). It reuses their telemetry bridge, battery, thermal,
     5	abort, agent and affinity stops, cache-drop handshake, owned-process stop, LMK query, PSS and power.
     6	
     7	VIS-3B (owner, 2026-10-22) replaced the multi-phase O,N,N,O design. Each launch runs one test in one configuration,
     8	saves it in its own folder and exits; nothing runs automatically after it. The 32 °C gate and the cooling wait are gone
     9	(the timed run `runs/20261022T153641Z` missed that gate at idle skin 32.95 °C). The genuine stops remain:
    10	battery ≥ 50 % Discharging, Android CRITICAL, battery ≥ 45 °C, owner abort, agent, cores 4–7, LMK, clean exit.
    11	Earlier folders in `runs/` are preserved as they are.
    12	
    13	## Why (diagnosis, read-only)
    14	
    15	The builds are equivalent: same Clang 21.1.8, `-O3`, `-march=armv8.2-a+dotprod+fp16`, OpenMP, REPACK, LLAMAFILE,
    16	shared libraries, no KleidiAI. Both use `sdot` (1045 vs 1117) and 456 FP16 `fmla .8h`. The effective runtime defaults
    17	also match: mmap (OLD default `mmap`, NEW `auto` → mmap on CPU), flash attention `auto` → enabled, batch 2048 / ubatch
    18	512, `--fit on`, `--swa-full` and `--cache-ram 0` honoured, and the MTP draft inherits the target's threads. The earlier
    19	OLD/NEW figures were taken at start skins between 24 and 39 °C with capped CPUs, so they do not isolate the build. Full
    20	evidence is in `reports/VIS3_CODER_REPORT.md`.
    21	
    22	## Tests
    23	
    24	| `--test` | Measures | Before the measurement |
    25	|---|---|---|
    26	| `cold_load` | spawn → `/health` ok, log-phase timestamps | page-cache drop (native handshake) |
    27	| `warm_load` | spawn → `/health` ok | every file the server reads, read once (`prime`) |
    28	| `first_response` | wall time of the first request (gen[0] prompt, 16 tokens) | prime, load, no warm-up |
    29	| `prompt` | 4 × (U ≈1005-token uncached, K cached prefix), 8 tokens each | prime, load, 1 discarded request |
    30	| `gen` | the 6 VIS-2 prompts, greedy, ≤ 128 tokens, no drafter | prime, load, 1 discarded request |
    31	| `mtp` | as `gen`, with the drafter (`--spec-draft-n-max 3`); draft acceptance | as `gen` |
    32	| `vision` | 4 VIS-1 frames through the Q8 projector, 70 image tokens (new build only) | prime, load, 1 discarded image |
    33	
    34	`--build old|new` is required. `--mtp` adds the drafter to any test except `gen` (use `--test mtp`). `--q8` adds the
    35	projector on the new build (implied by `vision`). The command is exactly `server_manager.start_setup('setup_q4')` (ctx
    36	2048, 4/4 threads, parallel 1, `--swa-full --cache-ram 0`), plus `taskset -c 4-7`, `timeout 900` and port 8091. Old and
    37	new differ only in the binary path (test-checked against `server_manager.py`).
    38	
    39	Each test checks:
    40	- `/props` and `/slots` (projector, drafter, n_ctx 2048);
    41	- `/proc/<pid>/maps` (only the build's own libraries);
    42	- hidden-setting environment variables (`LLAMA_*`, `GGML_*`, `OMP_*`, `KMP_*`, `LD_LIBRARY_PATH`, `LD_PRELOAD`), which
    43	  are refused.
    44	
    45	Each test records:
    46	- operating conditions at the start and end of the measurement: skin, Android status, battery °C, %, V and W, CPU caps,
    47	  MemAvailable, zone temperatures;
    48	- mean CPU frequency and time at top frequency (policy4/6, from cpufreq `time_in_state`) over the measurement and per
    49	  request;
    50	- PSS after load and at the end, peak PSS, minimum MemAvailable;
    51	- mean battery W, caps %, LMK, exit code and stop time.
    52	
    53	A test that already has a VALID result with identical configuration, code, payloads, weights and binaries is refused
    54	by preflight (`--repeat` to measure it again).
    55	
    56	## Owner commands (native Termux, no agent running, battery ≥ 50 % and unplugged, screen on, Termux in front)
    57	
    58	```bash
    59	O=~/robot/benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3
    60	bash $O/oneshot.sh --preflight --test gen --build old       # readiness for that test, starts nothing
    61	bash $O/oneshot.sh --test cold_load --build old
    62	bash $O/oneshot.sh --test cold_load --build new
    63	bash $O/oneshot.sh --test gen --build old
    64	bash $O/oneshot.sh --test gen --build new
    65	# likewise: warm_load, first_response, prompt, mtp (old/new), vision (new), --mtp / --q8 variants
    66	python3 -B $O/vis3_run.py --report $O/runs/<old folder> $O/runs/<new folder>
    67	```
    68	
    69	Expected time per launch: about 10 s for the monitor to start, then load 5–10 s and priming a few seconds.
    70	Measurement: load tests about 10 s, `first_response` about 3 s, `prompt` about 3 min, `gen`/`mtp` 1–2 min, `vision`
    71	about 1 min. For comparable pairs, run old and new back to back and alternate their order when repeating.
    72	
    73	Folders: `vis3/runs/<UTC>_<test>_<build>[_mtp][_q8][_repeat]/` with `run.json` (configuration, command, payload, code,
    74	weight and binary/library hashes, base commit, git status), `result.json`, `server.log`, `samples.jsonl`, `console.log`.
    75	
    76	`--report A B` prints both results, B/A ratios and `conditions`, stating whether the two ran under matched conditions:
    77	- same test, both VALID;
    78	- start skin within 1.0 °C, both at Android status 0;
    79	- battery within 10 %;
    80	- equal policy4/6 caps at the start.
    81	
    82	It also reports separately whether the measured mean frequencies are within 5 % (informational: they also reflect each
    83	build's own load), and whether prompt token counts and generated lengths are identical. Unmatched pairs are labelled as
    84	such, not discarded. Short tests do not establish thermal equilibrium (#93). These are Termux prototype results, not
    85	the Android app.

    20	@contextlib.contextmanager
    21	def patched(*triples):
    22	    saved = [(obj, name, getattr(obj, name)) for obj, name, _ in triples]
    23	    try:
    24	        for obj, name, value in triples:
    25	            setattr(obj, name, value)
    26	        yield
    27	    finally:
    28	        for obj, name, value in saved:
    29	            setattr(obj, name, value)
    30	
    31	
    32	def deployed_command(setup):
    33	    """The exact shell command server_manager.start_setup builds; os.system and waits are stubbed."""
    34	    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO))
    35	    import server_manager as sm
    36	    calls = []
    37	    with patched((sm.os, 'system', calls.append), (sm.time, 'sleep', lambda s: None),
    38	                 (sm, 'wait_for_server', lambda port=8080, timeout=60: True), (sm, 'mtp_draft_ok', lambda: True)):
    39	        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
    40	            sm.start_setup(setup)
    41	    return shlex.split(calls[-1].replace(' 2>/dev/null &', ''))
    42	
    43	
    44	def test_config():
    45	    assert v.config('gen', 'old') == dict(test='gen', build='old', mtp=False, q8=False)
    46	    assert v.config('mtp', 'new')['mtp'] and v.config('vision', 'new')['q8']
    47	    assert v.config('prompt', 'new', mtp=True, q8=True) == dict(test='prompt', build='new', mtp=True, q8=True)
    48	    for bad in (('gen', 'old', True, False), ('vision', 'old', False, False), ('cold_load', 'old', False, True),
    49	                ('x', 'old', False, False), ('gen', 'b1609', False, False)):
    50	        try:
    51	            v.config(*bad)
    52	        except ValueError:
    53	            continue
    54	        raise AssertionError(bad)
    55

    57	def test_commands_match_deployed_flags():
    58	    prefix = ['taskset', '-c', '4-7', 'timeout', '--signal=TERM', '--kill-after=3', '900']
    59	    for cfg, setup in ((v.config('gen', 'old'), 'setup_q4'), (v.config('mtp', 'old'), 'setup_q4_mtp')):
    60	        cmd = v.command(cfg)
    61	        want = deployed_command(setup)
    62	        want[want.index('--port') + 1] = '8091'
    63	        assert cmd[:7] == prefix and cmd[7:] == want, (cmd[7:], want)
    64	    old, new = v.command(v.config('gen', 'old')), v.command(v.config('gen', 'new'))
    65	    assert old[7] == str(v1.BINS['b1609-dotprod']) and new[7] == str(v1.BINS['vis'])
    66	    assert old[:7] + old[8:] == new[:7] + new[8:], 'only the binary may differ between old and new'
    67	    for test in v.TESTS[:5]:
    68	        assert v.command(v.config(test, 'new')) == new, 'every plain test launches the same server'
    69	    vis = v.command(v.config('vision', 'new'))
    70	    assert vis == new + ['--mmproj', str(v1.Q8), '--image-min-tokens', '70', '--image-max-tokens', '70']
    71	    assert v.command(v.config('mtp', 'new')) == new + v1.DRAFT_ARGS
    72	
    73	
    74	def test_payloads_per_test():
    75	    seqs = {t: v.payloads(v.config(t, 'new')) for t in v.TESTS}
    76	    assert seqs['cold_load'] == seqs['warm_load'] == (None, [])
    77	    warm, seq = seqs['first_response']
    78	    assert warm is None and len(seq) == 1 and seq[0][0] == 'first' and seq[0][3]['n_predict'] == v.FIRST_N
    79	    warm, seq = seqs['prompt']
    80	    assert warm[0] == 'first' and [k for k, *_ in seq] == ['uncached', 'cached'] * v.ROUNDS
    81	    assert all(b['cache_prompt'] is False for k, _, _, b in seq if k == 'uncached')
    82	    assert all(b['cache_prompt'] for k, _, _, b in seq if k == 'cached')
    83	    assert seqs['gen'] == seqs['mtp'] and len(seqs['gen'][1]) == len(v2.GEN_IDS)
    84	    for _, _, _, body in seqs['gen'][1]:
    85	        assert body['temperature'] == 0 and body['n_predict'] == v2.GEN_N and body['cache_prompt'] is False
    86	    assert seqs['gen'][1][0][3]['n_predict'] == v2.GEN_N, 'first-response body must not mutate gen[0]'
    87	    warm, seq = seqs['vision']

   173	def fake_run(tmp, cfg, fail_verify=False, libs_ok=True):
   174	    out = Path(tmp)
   175	    calls = dict(drop=0, prime=0, posts=[], stops=[])
   176	    tele = dict(skin=30., status=0, bat_c=28., capacity=70, voltage_now_uV=4e6, mem_available_kib=1,
   177	                max=dict(policy4=2348000, policy6=2850000), t=1.)
   178	
   179	    def launch(cmd, logpath, bridge, live):
   180	        live.update(proc=Proc(), pid=99)
   181	        logpath.write_text(T1_LOG.read_text(errors='replace'))
   182	        return 1.5
   183	
   184	    def timed(bridge, pid, logpath, kind, name, endpoint, body, timeout):
   185	        return dict(kind=kind, input=name, wall_ms=100., prompt_ms=50., prompt_tokens=10, generated_tokens=5,
   186	                    predicted_per_second=10., prompt_per_second=200., cache_n=0 if kind != 'cached' else 9,
   187	                    reply='ok', draft_n=None, draft_n_accepted=None, freq=None)
   188	
   189	    def verify(c):
   190	        if fail_verify:
   191	            raise RuntimeError('configuration mismatch')
   192	        return dict(vision=c['q8'], speculative=[c['mtp']], n_ctx=[2048], build_info='b')
   193	    freq = dict(policy4={2348000: 10}, policy6={2850000: 10})
   194	    with patched((v, 'launch', launch), (v, 'timed', timed), (v, 'verify', verify),
   195	                 (v, 'mapped_libs', lambda pid, b: dict(libs=['/elsewhere/libllama.so'], ok=libs_ok)),
   196	                 (v, 'drop_caches', lambda b: calls.__setitem__('drop', calls['drop'] + 1)),
   197	                 (v, 'prime', lambda paths: calls.__setitem__('prime', calls['prime'] + 1) or dict(bytes=1, s=0)),
   198	                 (v, 'freq_state', lambda root=None: freq),
   199	                 (v2, 'telemetry', lambda b: tele), (v2, 'fresh_pss', lambda b, p: dict(pss_kib=2048)),
   200	                 (v2, 'guarded_post', lambda b, p, e, body, t: calls['posts'].append(e) or {}),
   201	                 (v1, 'check_cores', lambda pid: None), (v1, 'agent_found', lambda log=False: False),
   202	                 (v1, 'own_stop', lambda proc, pid=None: calls['stops'].append(pid) or 0),
   203	                 (v1, 'group_alive', lambda g: False), (v1, 'lmk_request', lambda b, e: dict(ok=True)),
   204	                 (v1, 'metrics', lambda r: {})):
   205	        rec = v.run_test(cfg, out, out)
   206	        saved = json.loads((out/'result.json').read_text())
   207	        target = json.loads((out/'target.json').read_text())
   208	    return rec, calls, saved, target

   242	    spec = dict(config=dict(test='gen'), command=['x'], payload_sha256={}, code_sha256={'a': 1}, weight_sha256={},
   243	                binary_sha256={}, constants={}, git_status='?? other')

   262	def test_preflight_refusals():
   263	    cfg = v.config('gen', 'new')
   264	    spec = dict(weight_sha256={str(p): h for p, h in v.WEIGHTS.items()})
   265	    real = v.importlib.util.find_spec
   266	    base = ((v1, 'free_ports', lambda: None), (v1, 'agent_matches', lambda: []), (v, 'check_env', lambda: None),
   267	            (v, 'manifest', lambda c: spec))
   268	    with patched(*base, (v.importlib.util, 'find_spec', lambda m: None if m == 'requests' else real(m))):
   269	        try:
   270	            with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()) as out:
   271	                v.preflight(cfg)
   272	        except SystemExit as e:
   273	            assert 'requests' in str(e) and 'PREFLIGHT OK' not in out.getvalue()
   274	        else:
   275	            raise AssertionError('preflight must refuse without requests')
   276	    with patched(*base, (v, 'done_before', lambda s: Path('runs/prior'))):
   277	        try:
   278	            v.preflight(cfg)
   279	        except SystemExit as e:
   280	            assert 'already measured' in str(e)
   281	        else:
   282	            raise AssertionError('preflight must refuse a repeated VALID test')
   283	        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()) as out:
   284	            v.preflight(cfg, repeat=True)
   285	        assert 'PREFLIGHT OK' in out.getvalue()

   294	                start=start, measure_start_snapshot=start, end=dict(skin=skin + 3), libs=dict(ok=True),
   295	                pss_after_load=dict(pss_kib=3 << 20), exit_clean=True,
   296	                freq={p: dict(ticks=10, mean_mhz=mhz, top_share=1.) for p in v.POLICIES},

   300	def report_of(a, b):
   301	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   302	        for name, rec in (('a', a), ('b', b)):
   303	            Path(tmp, name).mkdir()
   304	            Path(tmp, name, 'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(rec))
   305	        out = io.StringIO()
   306	        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
   307	            lines = v.report(Path(tmp, 'a'), Path(tmp, 'b'))
   308	    return lines, json.loads(out.getvalue().splitlines()[-1])
   309

   311	def test_report_states_whether_conditions_match():
   312	    lines, last = report_of(result('old', 30., 12.), result('new', 30.6, 9.))
   313	    assert lines[0]['gen_tok_s'] == 12 and lines[1]['pss_after_load_mib'] == 3072 and lines[0]['skin_end'] == 33.
   314	    assert last['second_over_first']['gen_tok_s'] == .75 and last['prompt_n_identical'] and last['gen_tokens_identical']
   315	    c = last['conditions']
   316	    assert c['matched'] and c['measured_freq_within'] and c['start_skin'] == [30., 30.6]
   317	    for b, failed in ((result('new', 31.5, 9.), 'start_skin_within'), (result('new', 30., 9., capacity=55), 'battery_within'),
   318	                      (result('new', 30., 9., caps=1582000), 'start_caps_equal')):
   319	        _, last = report_of(result('old', 30., 12.), b)
   320	        assert not last['conditions']['matched'] and not last['conditions'][failed], failed
   321	    _, last = report_of(result('old', 30., 12.), result('new', 30., 9., mhz=1800))
   322	    assert last['conditions']['matched'] and not last['conditions']['measured_freq_within']
   323	    other = result('new', 30., 9.)
   324	    other['measure_start_snapshot'] = dict(other['start'], max=dict(other['start']['max'], policy0=5))
   325	    assert report_of(result('old', 30., 12.), other)[1]['conditions']['start_caps_equal'], 'little cores ignored'

     9	import json
    10	import os
    11	from pathlib import Path
    12	import signal
    13	import statistics
    14	import subprocess
    15	import sys

    20	sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
    21	HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    22	REPO = HERE.parents[2]
    23	_spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('vis1_run', REPO/'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis1/vis1_run.py')
    24	v1 = importlib.util.module_from_spec(_spec)
    25	_spec.loader.exec_module(v1)
    26	
    27	NATIVE = v1.NATIVE
    28	BIN = v1.BINS['vis']
    29	LIBS = sorted((BIN.parent).glob('lib*.so*'))  # llama-server is a 6 KB launcher; the code is in these
    30	WEIGHTS = {p: v1.EXPECTED_HASHES[p] for p in (v1.MODEL, v1.DRAFT, v1.Q8)}
    31	CONFIGS = dict(A=dict(mtp=True, q8=False), B=dict(mtp=True, q8=True),
    32	               C=dict(mtp=False, q8=False), D=dict(mtp=False, q8=True))
    33	BLOCKS = ('A', 'B', 'C', 'D', 'M')  # M = mixed text/image check on config B
    34	ROUNDS = 6           # timed: 6 x (uncached, cached, generation) per block; ABBA gives 12 per config
    35	BLOCK_CAP_S = 600    # measurement cap per block; the server itself is capped at 900 s by timeout
    36	REQ_TIMEOUT_S = 120
    37	GEN_N = 128
    38	GEN_IDS = v1.IDS[:ROUNDS]
    39	IMAGE_FRAME = 'bench_photos__person_facing_150cm'
    40	SMOKE_LABEL = 'SMOKE — NOT VALID TIMING'
    41	DRY_LABEL = 'NOT VALID TIMING (offline dry-run; no servers, gates or measurements)'
    42	# Fixed ~600-token robot log: a deterministic long prompt for prompt-processing throughput.
    43	LOG = '\n'.join(f'Cycle {i}: front {40 + 7*i % 160} cm clear, left wall {60 + 11*i % 90} cm, '
    44	                f'right door {"open" if i % 3 else "closed"}, person {"none" if i % 4 else "far left"}, '
    45	                f'battery {80 - i // 4}%.' for i in range(30))
    46	
    47	
    48	def config_of(block):
    49	    return CONFIGS['B' if block == 'M' else block]
    50	
    51	
    52	def command(block):
    53	    c = config_of(block)
    54	    cmd = ['taskset', '-c', '4-7', 'timeout', '--signal=TERM', '--kill-after=3', '900', str(BIN),
    55	           '-m', str(v1.MODEL), '--host', '127.0.0.1', '--port', '8091', '--ctx-size', '4096',
    56	           '--threads', '4', '--threads-batch', '4', '--parallel', '1', '--swa-full', '--cache-ram', '0',
    57	           '--reasoning', 'off']
    58	    if c['mtp']:
    59	        cmd += v1.DRAFT_ARGS
    60	    if c['q8']:
    61	        cmd += ['--mmproj', str(v1.Q8), '--image-min-tokens', '70', '--image-max-tokens', '70']
    62	    return cmd
    63	
    64	
    65	def text(user, n_predict, cache):
    66	    """Gemma raw prompt via chat.py (TPL-FIX, thinking off); greedy so A/B outputs can match."""
    67	    body = v1.text_payload(user)
    68	    body.update(temperature=0, top_k=1, seed=0, n_predict=n_predict, cache_prompt=cache)
    69	    return body
    70	
    71	
    72	def payloads():
    73	    bench = v1.module('vis2_bench', 'benchmark/llm_objective_setting/bench.py')
    74	    by_id = {p['id']: p for p in bench.PROMPTS_A + bench.PROMPTS_B + bench.PROMPTS_C}
    75	    gen = [(i, text(' '.join(t['user'] for t in by_id[i]['text']) if by_id[i].get('multi')
    76	                    else by_id[i]['text'], GEN_N, False)) for i in GEN_IDS]
    77	    unc = ('U', text(LOG + '\nSummarize the log in one sentence.', 8, False))
    78	    cached = [(f'K{k}', text(LOG + f'\nIn one sentence: what happened at cycle {k}?', 8, True))
    79	              for k in range(ROUNDS)]
    80	    return gen, unc, cached
    81	
    82	
    83	def image_payload():
    84	    _, images = v1.inputs()
    85	    return dict(images)[IMAGE_FRAME]
    86	
    87	
    88	def sequence(block, smoke, gen, unc, cached, image=None):
    89	    """(kind, name, endpoint, body). Fixed order; identical between matched configs."""
    90	    if block == 'M':
    91	        g = gen[0]
    92	        seq = [('gen', *g), ('uncached', *unc), ('image', IMAGE_FRAME, image), ('gen', *g), ('uncached', *unc)]
    93	    else:
    94	        seq = []
    95	        for k in range(1 if smoke else ROUNDS):
    96	            seq += [('uncached', *unc), ('cached', *cached[k]), ('gen', *gen[k])]
    97	    return [(kind, name, '/v1/chat/completions' if kind == 'image' else '/completion', body)
    98	            for kind, name, body in seq]
    99	
   100	
   101	def get(path):
   102	    with urllib.request.urlopen('http://127.0.0.1:8091'+path, timeout=10) as r:
   103	        return json.load(r)
   104	
   105	
   106	def verify_config(block):
   107	    """The running server must report the intended projector and MTP state; never adjust flags."""
   108	    c = config_of(block)
   109	    props, slots = get('/props'), get('/slots')
   110	    vision = props.get('modalities', {}).get('vision')
   111	    spec = [s.get('speculative') for s in slots]
   112	    got = dict(vision=vision, speculative=spec)
   113	    if vision is not c['q8'] or spec != [c['mtp']]:
   114	        raise RuntimeError(f'configuration mismatch: expected q8={c["q8"]} mtp={c["mtp"]}, server reports {got}')
   115	    return got
   116	
   117	
   118	def fresh_pss(bridge, pid, wait=5):
   119	    """PSS of this server from a monitor sample begun after now, taken while the server is idle."""
   120	    after, deadline = time.monotonic(), time.monotonic() + wait
   121	    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
   122	        for s in reversed(v1.rows(bridge/'samples.jsonl')):
   123	            if s.get('t_start', -1) <= after:
   124	                break
   125	            if s.get('pid') == pid:
   126	                return dict(pss_kib=s.get('pss_kib'), t=s['t'], after=after)
   127	        time.sleep(.2)
   128	    return dict(pss_kib=None, t=None, after=after, unavailable=f'no fresh sample within {wait} s')
   129	
   130	
   131	def telemetry(bridge):
   132	    """Marked copy of vis1_run.telemetry (VIS2-BATTERY-FIX): no 80 % upper bound; all other stops kept."""
   133	    if (bridge/'abort').exists():
   134	        raise SystemExit('owner aborted wrapper')
   135	    d = json.loads((bridge/'latest.json').read_text())
   136	    if time.monotonic() - d['t'] > 12:
   137	        raise RuntimeError('native telemetry stale')
   138	    if d.get('error'):
   139	        raise RuntimeError('native telemetry failed: ' + d['error'])
   140	    if d['capacity'] < 50 or d['battery_status'] != 'Discharging':
   141	        raise RuntimeError('battery below 50% or not Discharging')
   142	    if d['agent']:
   143	        v1.log_agent_matches(d.get('agent_detail', []))
   144	        raise RuntimeError('agent found')
   145	    if d['skin'] is None or d['status'] is None:
   146	        raise RuntimeError('missing VIRTUAL-SKIN or Android status')
   147	    if d['status'] >= 4 or d['bat_c'] >= 45:
   148	        raise RuntimeError('thermal stop (Android CRITICAL or battery >=45 C)')
   149	    return d
   150	
   151	
   152	def gate(bridge, idle):
   153	    """Marked copy of vis1_run.gate using the local telemetry()."""
   154	    start = time.monotonic()
   155	    while True:
   156	        d = telemetry(bridge)
   157	        if v1.gate_ok(d['skin'], idle):
   158	            return dict(skin=d['skin'], status=d['status'], waited_s=time.monotonic()-start, missed=False)
   159	        if time.monotonic()-start >= 900:
   160	            raise RuntimeError('gate missed: not cool after 15 minutes; no warm-start block')
   161	        time.sleep(5)
   162	
   163	
   164	def guarded_post(bridge, pid, endpoint, body, timeout):
   165	    """Request in a worker; telemetry/abort/affinity stay enforced every 0.5 s while it runs.
   166	    A violation raises here and run_block's finally stops the owned server, ending the request."""
   167	    result = {}
   168	
   169	    def work():
   170	        try:
   171	            result['data'] = v1.post(endpoint, body, timeout=timeout)
   172	        except BaseException as e:
   173	            result['error'] = e
   174	    th = threading.Thread(target=work, daemon=True)
   175	    th.start()
   176	    while th.is_alive():
   177	        th.join(.5)
   178	        if th.is_alive():
   179	            telemetry(bridge)
   180	            v1.check_cores(pid)
   181	    if 'error' in result:
   182	        raise result['error']
   183	    return result['data']
   184	
   185	
   186	def sample_violations(samples):
   187	    """Every recorded sample must satisfy the same stops telemetry() enforces live."""
   188	    bad = []
   189	    if any(s.get('agent') for s in samples):
   190	        bad.append('agent')
   191	    if any(s.get('battery_status') != 'Discharging' or s.get('capacity', -1) < 50 for s in samples):
   192	        bad.append('battery band')
   193	    if any(s.get('cores_ok') is False for s in samples):
   194	        bad.append('affinity')
   195	    if any((s.get('status') or 0) >= 4 or (s.get('bat_c') or 0) >= 45 for s in samples):
   196	        bad.append('thermal stop threshold')
   197	    return bad
   198	
   199	
   200	def run_block(block, index, out, bridge, idle, seq, smoke):
   201	    record = dict(phase_start=time.monotonic(), block=block, index=index, config=config_of(block),
   202	                  label=SMOKE_LABEL if smoke else 'NOT VALID', reason='interrupted', requests=[],
   203	                  command=command(block), started_epoch=time.time(), smoke=smoke)
   204	    attempt = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ')
   205	    path = out/f'{index:02d}_{block}_{attempt}.json'
   206	    logpath = out/f'{index:02d}_{block}_{attempt}.server.log'
   207	    proc = pid = None
   208	    try:
   209	        if not smoke:
   210	            record['gate'] = gate(bridge, idle)
   211	        v1.free_ports()
   212	        if v1.agent_found(log=True):
   213	            raise RuntimeError('agent found before launch')
   214	        if not smoke:  # cold load: same native cache-drop handshake as VIS-1
   215	            (bridge/'drop_done').unlink(missing_ok=True)
   216	            (bridge/'drop_request').touch()
   217	            began = time.monotonic()
   218	            while not (bridge/'drop_done').exists():
   219	                if time.monotonic()-began > 30:
   220	                    raise RuntimeError('cache-drop handshake timeout')
   221	                time.sleep(.1)
   222	            if (bridge/'drop_done').read_text().strip() != 'ok':
   223	                raise RuntimeError('cache drop failed')
   224	            (bridge/'drop_request').unlink()
   225	            (bridge/'drop_done').unlink()
   226	            record['gate_after_drop'] = gate(bridge, idle)
   227	        d = telemetry(bridge)
   228	        record['battery_start'] = dict(capacity=d['capacity'], voltage_now_uV=d['voltage_now_uV'], t=d['t'])
   229	        with logpath.open('w') as log:
   230	            began = time.monotonic()
   231	            proc = v1.spawn(record['command'], log)
   232	            while pid is None:
   233	                if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic()-began > 10:
   234	                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed or owned child not found')
   235	                try:
   236	                    pid = v1.server_pid(proc)
   237	                except (OSError, RuntimeError):
   238	                    time.sleep(.05)
   239	            v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=pid))
   240	            while True:
   241	                if proc.poll() is not None:
   242	                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed during load')
   243	                try:
   244	                    if v1.post('/health', timeout=1).get('status') == 'ok':
   245	                        break
   246	                except (OSError, ValueError):
   247	                    pass
   248	                telemetry(bridge)
   249	                if time.monotonic()-began >= 180:
   250	                    raise RuntimeError('server load timeout')
   251	                time.sleep(.2)
   252	            record.update(server_pid=pid, load_s=time.monotonic()-began, loaded_at=time.monotonic())
   253	            v1.check_cores(pid)
   254	            record['reported'] = verify_config(block)
   255	            record['pss_after_load'] = fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
   256	            warm = next(x for x in seq if x[0] == 'gen')
   257	            guarded_post(bridge, pid, warm[2], warm[3], REQ_TIMEOUT_S)  # discarded warm-up (generation)
   258	            record['measure_start'] = start = time.monotonic()
   259	            for kind, name, endpoint, body in seq:
   260	                telemetry(bridge)
   261	                v1.check_cores(pid)
   262	                left = BLOCK_CAP_S - (time.monotonic()-start)
   263	                if left <= 0:
   264	                    raise RuntimeError(f'block cap {BLOCK_CAP_S} s exceeded')
   265	                if kind == 'image':
   266	                    record['pss_before_image'] = fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
   267	                offset = logpath.stat().st_size
   268	                t = time.monotonic()
   269	                data = guarded_post(bridge, pid, endpoint, body, min(REQ_TIMEOUT_S, left))
   270	                end = time.monotonic()
   271	                if end-start > BLOCK_CAP_S:
   272	                    raise RuntimeError(f'block cap {BLOCK_CAP_S} s exceeded')
   273	                if kind == 'image':
   274	                    record['pss_after_image'] = fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
   275	                with logpath.open() as f:
   276	                    f.seek(offset)
   277	                    delta = f.read()
   278	                row = v1.request_record(name, data, t, end, delta, 'V2' if kind == 'image' else 'T2')
   279	                row.update(kind=kind, cache_n=row['timings'].get('cache_n'),
   280	                           prompt_per_second=row['timings'].get('prompt_per_second'))
   281	                record['requests'].append(row)
   282	                v1.write(path, record)
   283	            record['measure_end'] = time.monotonic()
   284	            if proc.poll() is not None:
   285	                raise RuntimeError('server crashed during measurement')
   286	            v1.check_cores(pid)
   287	            telemetry(bridge)
   288	            record.update(label=SMOKE_LABEL if smoke else 'VALID', reason='completed fixed request sequence')
   289	    except (Exception, SystemExit) as e:
   290	        record.update(label='NOT VALID', reason=str(e))
   291	    finally:
   292	        record['stop_start'] = time.monotonic()
   293	        v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=None))
   294	        if proc is not None:
   295	            record['server_exit'] = v1.own_stop(proc, pid)
   296	            record['exit_clean'] = record['server_exit'] == 0 and not v1.group_alive(proc.pid)
   297	        record['stopped'] = time.monotonic()
   298	        record['samples'] = [s for s in v1.rows(bridge/'samples.jsonl')
   299	                             if record['phase_start']-1 <= s['t'] <= record['stopped']]
   300	        record['lmk'] = v1.lmk_request(bridge, record['started_epoch'])
   301	        record['metrics'] = v1.metrics(record)
   302	        if record['label'] != 'NOT VALID':  # VALID and SMOKE both need a clean finish
   303	            bad = []
   304	            if proc is not None and not record['exit_clean']:
   305	                bad.append(f"server cleanup not clean (exit {record['server_exit']})")
   306	            if not record['lmk'].get('ok'):
   307	                bad.append('LMK query failed')
   308	            if sample_violations(record['samples']):
   309	                bad.append('sample violation: ' + ', '.join(sample_violations(record['samples'])))
   310	            if [e for e in v1.rows(bridge/'errors.jsonl') if record['phase_start'] <= e['t'] <= record['stop_start']]:
   311	                bad.append('sampling failed')
   312	            if bad:
   313	                record.update(label='NOT VALID', reason='; '.join(bad))
   314	        v1.write(path, record)
   315	    return record
   316	
   317	
   318	def blocks(arg):
   319	    sel = arg.split(',')
   320	    if not sel or any(b not in BLOCKS for b in sel):
   321	        raise ValueError('--blocks takes a comma list of A,B,C,D,M (repeats allowed, e.g. A,B,B,A)')
   322	    return sel

   325	def code_paths():
   326	    return ['benchmark/gemma_vision/vis2/vis2_run.py', 'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis2/oneshot.sh',
   327	            'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis1/vis1_run.py', 'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis0/vis0.py',
   328	            'benchmark/llm_objective_setting/bench.py', 'benchmark/campaign/power.py',
   329	            'benchmark/campaign/runtime.py', 'chat.py', 'server_manager.py']
   330	
   331	
   332	def manifest(selected, smoke, gen, unc, cached, image):
   333	    allp = gen + [unc] + cached + ([(IMAGE_FRAME, image)] if image else [])
   334	    return dict(blocks=selected, smoke=smoke, rounds=ROUNDS, gen_n=GEN_N, block_cap_s=BLOCK_CAP_S,
   335	                commands={b: command(b) for b in sorted(set(selected))},
   336	                payload_sha256={n: hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(p, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest() for n, p in allp},
   337	                code_sha256={p: v1.sha(REPO/p) for p in code_paths()},
   338	                weight_sha256={str(p): v1.sha(p) for p in WEIGHTS},
   339	                binary_sha256={str(p): v1.sha(p) for p in [BIN] + LIBS},
   340	                base_commit=subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=REPO, text=True).strip(),
   341	                git_status=subprocess.check_output(['git', 'status', '--porcelain=v1'], cwd=REPO, text=True))
   342	
   343	
   344	def preflight():
   345	    """Before and after the 5-minute idle: ports, agents, files/hashes, plus Debian imports or native root/battery/cpuset."""
   346	    v1.free_ports()
   347	    matches = v1.agent_matches()
   348	    if matches:
   349	        v1.log_agent_matches(matches)
   350	        raise SystemExit('agent found; close Claude/AGY/Codex (see pid/comm/cmdline above)')
   351	    missing = [str(p) for p in (*WEIGHTS, BIN) if not p.is_file()]
   352	    if missing or not LIBS:
   353	        raise SystemExit('missing file(s): ' + ', '.join(missing or ['llama.cpp-vis shared libraries']))
   354	    bad = [str(p) for p, h in WEIGHTS.items() if v1.sha(p) != h]
   355	    if bad:
   356	        raise SystemExit('SHA-256 mismatch: ' + ', '.join(bad))
   357	    if Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
   358	        missing_mods = [m for m in v1.DEBIAN_IMPORTS if importlib.util.find_spec(m) is None]
   359	        if missing_mods:
   360	            raise SystemExit('Debian python3 is missing ' + ', '.join(missing_mods))
   361	    else:
   362	        if subprocess.run(['su', '-c', 'id'], capture_output=True, timeout=10).returncode != 0:
   363	            raise SystemExit('no root (su) access; telemetry needs root')
   364	        cap, status = v1.root(f'cat {v1.BAT}/capacity {v1.BAT}/status', 'preflight_battery').split()[:2]
   365	        if not (cap.isdigit() and int(cap) >= 50 and status == 'Discharging'):
   366	            raise SystemExit('battery below 50% or not Discharging; unplug the charger')
   367	        before = os.sched_getaffinity(0)
   368	        os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
   369	        stuck = os.sched_getaffinity(0) == {4, 5, 6, 7}
   370	        os.sched_setaffinity(0, before)
   371	        if not stuck:
   372	            raise SystemExit('Termux cpuset blocks cores 4-7; bring Termux to the foreground, screen on')
   373	    print('PREFLIGHT OK', flush=True)
   374	
   375	
   376	def mib(snap):
   377	    return snap['pss_kib'] and round(snap['pss_kib']/1024) if snap else None
   378	
   379	
   380	def med(xs):
   381	    xs = [x for x in xs if x is not None]
   382	    return round(statistics.median(xs), 2) if xs else None
   383	
   384	
   385	def summarize(folder):
   386	    """Per-block medians from saved records only; missing fields print as None (unavailable)."""
   387	    lines = []
   388	    for p in sorted(Path(folder).glob('[0-9][0-9]_[A-DM]_*Z.json')):
   389	        d = json.loads(p.read_text())
   390	        r, m = d['requests'], d.get('metrics', {})
   391	        by = lambda k: [x for x in r if x['kind'] == k]
   392	        dn = sum(x.get('draft_n') or 0 for x in by('gen'))
   393	        da = sum(x.get('draft_n_accepted') or 0 for x in by('gen'))
   394	        lines.append(dict(
   395	            block=f"{d['index']:02d}_{d['block']}", label=d['label'], reason=d['reason'], load_s=med([d.get('load_s')]),
   396	            battery_start=d.get('battery_start'),
   397	            reported=d.get('reported'), uncached_pp_tok_s=med([x['prompt_per_second'] for x in by('uncached')]),
   398	            uncached_prompt_n=sorted({x['prompt_tokens'] for x in by('uncached')}),
   399	            cached_ms=med([x['prompt_ms'] for x in by('cached')]),
   400	            cached_cache_n=sorted({x['cache_n'] for x in by('cached')}),
   401	            gen_tok_s=med([x['predicted_per_second'] for x in by('gen')]),
   402	            gen_tokens=[x['generated_tokens'] for x in by('gen')],
   403	            gen_wall_ms=med([x['wall_ms'] for x in by('gen')]),
   404	            draft_acceptance=round(da/dn, 3) if dn else None,
   405	            image=[dict(wall_ms=round(x['wall_ms']), prompt_tokens=x['prompt_tokens'], usage=x.get('usage'))
   406	                   for x in by('image')],
   407	            pss_after_load_mib=mib(d.get('pss_after_load')),
   408	            peak_pss_mib=m.get('peak_pss_kib') and round(m['peak_pss_kib']/1024),
   409	            pss_image_before_after_mib=[mib(d.get('pss_before_image')), mib(d.get('pss_after_image'))],
   410	            checks=dict(uncached_cache_n_zero=all(x['cache_n'] == 0 for x in by('uncached')),
   411	                        cached_reuse=bool(by('cached')) and all((x['cache_n'] or 0) > 0 for x in by('cached')),
   412	                        replies_nonempty=all((x.get('reply') or '').strip() for x in r)),
   413	            min_mem_available_mib=m.get('min_mem_available_kib') and round(m['min_mem_available_kib']/1024),
   414	            lmk=d.get('lmk'), server_exit=d.get('server_exit'), exit_clean=d.get('exit_clean'),
   415	            skin=[m.get('skin_start'), m.get('skin_end')], status_max=m.get('status_max'),
   416	            caps={k: round(v['percent']) for k, v in m.get('caps', {}).items()}, power=m.get('power')))
   417	    for line in lines:
   418	        print(json.dumps(line), flush=True)
   419	    # VALID covers execution conditions only; these say whether blocks are comparable at all.
   420	    timed = [x for x in lines if not x['block'].endswith('M') and x['label'] == 'VALID']
   421	    print(json.dumps(dict(comparability=dict(
   422	        gen_tokens_identical=len({tuple(x['gen_tokens']) for x in timed}) <= 1,
   423	        uncached_prompt_n_identical=len({tuple(x['uncached_prompt_n']) for x in timed}) <= 1,
   424	        all_checks_pass=all(all(x['checks'].values()) for x in lines)))), flush=True)
   425	    return lines
   426	
   427	
   428	def main(argv=None):
   429	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
   430	    ap.add_argument('--blocks', default='A,B,B,A')
   431	    ap.add_argument('--smoke', action='store_true')
   432	    ap.add_argument('--dry-run', action='store_true')
   433	    ap.add_argument('--summarize', type=Path)
   434	    ap.add_argument('--bridge', type=Path)
   435	    ap.add_argument('--preflight', action='store_true', help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
   436	    ap.add_argument('--monitor', type=Path, help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
   437	    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
   438	    if a.preflight:
   439	        return preflight()
   440	    if a.monitor:
   441	        return v1.monitor(a.monitor)
   442	    if a.summarize:
   443	        return summarize(a.summarize)
   444	    selected = blocks(a.blocks)
   445	    if a.dry_run:
   446	        gen, unc, cached = payloads()
   447	        print(DRY_LABEL)
   448	        print(json.dumps(dict(blocks=selected, commands={b: command(b) for b in sorted(set(selected))},
   449	                              sequence=[(b, [(k, n, e) for k, n, e, _ in sequence(b, a.smoke, gen, unc, cached, {})])
   450	                                        for b in sorted(set(selected))],
   451	                              uncached_chars=len(unc[1]['prompt'])), indent=1))
   452	        return
   453	    if a.bridge is None or not Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
   454	        raise SystemExit('launch only through native vis2/oneshot.sh into Debian')
   455	    out = bridge = a.bridge.resolve()
   456	    if not (bridge/'owner_launch').is_file():
   457	        raise SystemExit('missing owner wrapper receipt')
   458	    if v1.agent_found(log=True):
   459	        raise SystemExit('agent found; no server started')
   460	    v1.free_ports()
   461	    telemetry(bridge)
   462	    os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
   463	    v1.check_cores(os.getpid())
   464	    for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
   465	        signal.signal(sig, v1.interrupted)
   466	    gen, unc, cached = payloads()
   467	    image = image_payload() if 'M' in selected else None
   468	    if any(v1.sha(p) != h for p, h in WEIGHTS.items()):
   469	        raise SystemExit('model/projector/drafter SHA-256 mismatch')
   470	    idle = None if a.smoke else json.loads((bridge/'idle.json').read_text())['skin']
   471	    v1.write(out/'run.json', dict(manifest=manifest(selected, a.smoke, gen, unc, cached, image), idle_skin=idle,
   472	             note='Short blocks; thermal equivalence between blocks is not established (#93). '
   473	                  + (SMOKE_LABEL if a.smoke else 'ABBA order controls linear drift only.')))
   474	    print('RUN_FOLDER='+str(out), flush=True)
   475	    for i, b in enumerate(selected):
   476	        result = run_block(b, i, out, bridge, idle, sequence(b, a.smoke, gen, unc, cached, image), a.smoke)
   477	        print(f"{i:02d} {b}: {result['label']} — {result['reason']}", flush=True)
   478	        if result['label'] == 'NOT VALID':
   479	            raise SystemExit('stopped; later blocks not run. See README recovery.')
   480	    summarize(out)
   481	    print('Finished. Package RUN_FOLDER (see README); do not rerun for analysis.', flush=True)
   482	
   483	
   484	if __name__ == '__main__':
   485	    try:
   486	        main()
   487	    finally:
   488	        for proc in v1.OWNED:
   489	            v1.own_stop(proc)

    30	1. **P3 — Sampler detachment is best-effort, despite the README’s unconditional guarantee.**
    31	
    32	   `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py:197`:
    33	   ```python
    34	   def detach(bridge, wait=5):
    35	   ```
    36	
    37	   `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py:214`:
    38	   ```python
    39	           phase['sampler_detached'] = detach(bridge)
    40	   ```
    41	
    42	   `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py:216`:
    43	   ```python
    44	           phase['server_exit'] = v1.own_stop(proc, pid)
    45	   ```
    46	
    47	   A successful acknowledgement resolves the original race: the monitor samples sequentially, so a completed sample begun after clearing the target also establishes that the earlier sample finished. However, shutdown proceeds when acknowledgement times out.
    48	
    49	   Concrete scenario: a native sample stalls for more than five seconds before reading the cold server’s `/proc` files. Detachment returns false, shutdown removes those files, and the delayed sample records an error. The warm gate or final sampling-error check can then reject otherwise successful work. This remains fail-closed for measurement validity, so it is nonblocking.
    50	
    51	   Make acknowledgement failure stop the block explicitly after owned-server cleanup, and describe the bounded fallback accurately. A delayed-sample lifecycle test would cover this better than the current synthetic future-timestamp test.
    52	
    53	The other round 1 findings are addressed: relevant environment overrides are rejected before server launch; pooling compares commands; the warm phase receives its own thermal gate and starting-temperature summary; foreign mapped libraries immediately reject the block; and Debian preflight checks for `requests`.
    54	
    55	The whole-candidate review found matching OLD/NEW command arguments, payloads, sampling, affinity, context and output budgets. Cold loading retains the cache-drop handshake; warm loading avoids another drop. Thermal protections remain intact, with the additional absolute ceiling. Owned-process shutdown remains scoped to processes launched by the harness. Summaries exclude invalid/smoke blocks from configuration aggregates and expose comparability separately from execution validity.
    56	
    57	The diagnosis is broadly supported by the compiler records, relevant CPU build settings, archived help, draft-thread inheritance and NEW server/projector source. The literal claim that the CMake caches differ *only* in two settings is too broad: NEW also introduces cache entries, including inactive backend options. This does not establish a CPU comparison confound.
    58	
    59	The reload proposal was closed on August 11, and the eviction proposal remains a draft focused on GPU memory swapping. [Reload PR](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/25963), [eviction PR](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28346). I did not independently recount instruction totals, fully reproduce historical thermal figures, verify shallow-clone provenance, or verify issue #30776.
    60	
    61	Supplied outputs report VIS-3 17/17 passing, VIS-2 passing, and the disclosed pre-existing VIS-1 wrapper-contract error. My permitted VIS-3 rerun exited 182 without output; these test results were inspected but not independently reproduced. Hardware behavior and timed performance remain for the owner’s later runs.

   526	+def conditions(a, b):
   527	+    """Whether two results ran under matched operating conditions; each criterion reported separately."""

   534	+        start_status_normal=sa['status'] == sb['status'] == 0,
   535	+        battery_within=known(sa['capacity'], sb['capacity']) and abs(sa['capacity']-sb['capacity']) <= MATCH_BATTERY_PCT,

  1293	-differ only in `LLAMA_BUILD_IS_DEV` (version string) and `LLAMA_BUILD_UI`. The effective runtime defaults also match: mmap loading
  1294	-(OLD default `mmap`, NEW default `auto` = mmap on CPU, logged `load_mode = mmap`), flash attention `auto` (enabled), batch 2048,
  1295	-ubatch 512, `--fit on`, `--swa-full` and `--cache-ram 0` honoured. In `common_base_params_to_speculative`, the branch that
  1296	-gives the MTP draft the target's threads is the same in both builds, but NEW adds other behaviour to that function. b11716 creates an explicit ggml threadpool. Under OpenMP this
  1297	-is plausibly neutral, but that is unverified.

  1329	-cache drop and the gate again. **Cold phase:** launch, readiness, `/props` and `/slots` checks (no vision, intended MTP,
  1330	-n_ctx 2048), the mapped libraries in `/proc/<pid>/maps` must come from the block's own build, PSS, then the first response
  1331	-(gen[0] prompt, 16 tokens), then a clean stop. **Warm phase:** the gate again (no cache drop), relaunch (files still cached), the same checks
  1332	-and first response, then 4 rounds of U (≈1005-token uncached log, 8 tokens), K (cached prefix, 8 tokens) and G (VIS-1
  1333	-prompt, greedy, up to 128 tokens), then a clean stop. Requests are greedy (temperature 0, top_k 1, seed 0) and identical
  1334	-for every block.

  1337	-and generation tok/s, cache reuse, draft acceptance, and the mean CPU frequency per request from cpufreq `time_in_state`

  1339	-skin, Android status, CPU caps, LMK lines, and exit code and stop time.

  1342	-comparability checks: both builds present, balanced, identical prompt token counts (tokenization), identical generated
  1343	-lengths, cold and warm start-skin spread, and every check passing. The summary pools folders only when their code, payloads, weights, binaries, commands
  1344	-and constants match. Draw conclusions only after that assessment.
  1345	+| `cold_load` | spawn → `/health` ok, log-phase timestamps | page-cache drop (native handshake) |
  1346	+| `warm_load` | spawn → `/health` ok | every file the server reads, read once (`prime`) |
  1347	+| `first_response` | wall time of the first request (gen[0] prompt, 16 tokens) | prime, load, no warm-up |

    30	1. **P3 — Sampler detachment is best-effort, despite the README’s unconditional guarantee.**
    31	
    32	   `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py:197`:
    33	   ```python
    34	   def detach(bridge, wait=5):
    35	   ```
    36	
    37	   `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py:214`:
    38	   ```python
    39	           phase['sampler_detached'] = detach(bridge)
    40	   ```
    41	
    42	   `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py:216`:
    43	   ```python
    44	           phase['server_exit'] = v1.own_stop(proc, pid)
    45	   ```
    46	
    47	   A successful acknowledgement resolves the original race: the monitor samples sequentially, so a completed sample begun after clearing the target also establishes that the earlier sample finished. However, shutdown proceeds when acknowledgement times out.
    48	
    49	   Concrete scenario: a native sample stalls for more than five seconds before reading the cold server’s `/proc` files. Detachment returns false, shutdown removes those files, and the delayed sample records an error. The warm gate or final sampling-error check can then reject otherwise successful work. This remains fail-closed for measurement validity, so it is nonblocking.
    50	
    51	   Make acknowledgement failure stop the block explicitly after owned-server cleanup, and describe the bounded fallback accurately. A delayed-sample lifecycle test would cover this better than the current synthetic future-timestamp test.
    52	
    53	The other round 1 findings are addressed: relevant environment overrides are rejected before server launch; pooling compares commands; the warm phase receives its own thermal gate and starting-temperature summary; foreign mapped libraries immediately reject the block; and Debian preflight checks for `requests`.
    54	
    55	The whole-candidate review found matching OLD/NEW command arguments, payloads, sampling, affinity, context and output budgets. Cold loading retains the cache-drop handshake; warm loading avoids another drop. Thermal protections remain intact, with the additional absolute ceiling. Owned-process shutdown remains scoped to processes launched by the harness. Summaries exclude invalid/smoke blocks from configuration aggregates and expose comparability separately from execution validity.
    56	
    57	The diagnosis is broadly supported by the compiler records, relevant CPU build settings, archived help, draft-thread inheritance and NEW server/projector source. The literal claim that the CMake caches differ *only* in two settings is too broad: NEW also introduces cache entries, including inactive backend options. This does not establish a CPU comparison confound.
    58	
    59	The reload proposal was closed on August 11, and the eviction proposal remains a draft focused on GPU memory swapping. [Reload PR](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/25963), [eviction PR](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28346). I did not independently recount instruction totals, fully reproduce historical thermal figures, verify shallow-clone provenance, or verify issue #30776.

    54	def module(name, relative):
    55	    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, REPO / relative)

    57	    spec.loader.exec_module(m)

    61	def sha(path):
    62	    with Path(path).open('rb') as f:
    63	        return hashlib.file_digest(f, 'sha256').hexdigest()
    64

    66	def write(path, value):
    67	    path = Path(path)
    68	    tmp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + '.tmp')
    69	    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2, allow_nan=False) + '\n')
    70	    tmp.replace(path)
    71	
    72	
    73	def append(path, value):
    74	    with Path(path).open('a') as f:
    75	        f.write(json.dumps(value, allow_nan=False) + '\n')
    76	        f.flush()
