    79	def rows(path):
    80	    if not Path(path).exists():
    81	        return []
    82	    lines = Path(path).read_text().splitlines()
    83	    out = []
    84	    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
    85	        try:
    86	            out.append(json.loads(line))
    87	        except json.JSONDecodeError:
    88	            if i != len(lines)-1:
    89	                raise
    90	    return out

    93	def blocks(only):

    95	    if len(set(selected)) != len(selected) or set(selected) - set(ORDER):
    96	        raise ValueError('--only requires unique block names T1,T2,V1,V2,V3,V4')
    97	    return [b for b in ORDER if b in selected]

   158	def own_stop(proc, server=None):
   159	    """Only the private process group created by our Popen; timeout is a second hard bound."""
   160	    if proc.poll() is None:
   161	        # VIS1-FIX3: GNU timeout re-sends a TERM it receives to its whole group (kill(0, sig)), so
   162	        # TERM to timeout reached llama-server twice ("second interrupt" -> abort). TERM the owned
   163	        # server once (still our direct child in our group); timeout then exits with its status.
   164	        # Server not known yet: fall back to timeout.
   165	        owned = server is not None and (server, proc.pid, proc.pid) in set(proc_rows())
   166	        try:
   167	            os.kill(server if owned else proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
   168	            proc.wait(timeout=3)
   169	        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   170	            os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
   171	            proc.wait(timeout=5)
   172	        except ProcessLookupError:
   173	            proc.wait(timeout=5)
   174	    return proc.returncode
   175

   177	OWNED = []
   178	DEFERRED = dict(on=False, signum=None)
   179	
   180	
   181	def interrupted(n, frame):
   182	    if DEFERRED['on']:
   183	        DEFERRED['signum'] = n
   184	    else:
   185	        raise SystemExit(f'interrupted {n}')
   186	
   187	
   188	def spawn(cmd, log):
   189	    DEFERRED.update(on=True, signum=None)
   190	    try:
   191	        proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
   192	                                start_new_session=True)
   193	        OWNED.append(proc)
   194	    finally:
   195	        DEFERRED['on'] = False
   196	    if DEFERRED['signum'] is not None:
   197	        own_stop(proc)
   198	        raise SystemExit(f'interrupted {DEFERRED["signum"]}')
   199	    return proc
   200	
   201	
   202	AGENT_NAMES = {'claude', 'codex', 'agy'}
   203	
   204	
   205	def agent_matches():
   206	    """Processes whose own binary is claude/codex/agy, not merely a path or argument containing those
   207	    letters (VIS1-FIX: pgrep -f alone matches substrings anywhere in argv, e.g. a --config path under
   208	    ~/.codex/). pgrep -f still casts the wide net; comm/argv0 narrows it to real agent processes,
   209	    including a lingering app-server-daemon left behind after the owner closes the interactive CLI."""
   210	    p = subprocess.run(['pgrep', '-f', 'claude|codex|agy'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
   211	    if p.returncode not in (0, 1):
   212	        raise RuntimeError('agent process check failed')
   213	    found = []
   214	    for token in p.stdout.split():
   215	        pid = int(token)
   216	        if pid == os.getpid():
   217	            continue
   218	        try:
   219	            comm = Path(f'/proc/{pid}/comm').read_text().strip()
   220	            parts = Path(f'/proc/{pid}/cmdline').read_bytes().split(b'\0')
   221	            cmdline = ' '.join(c.decode(errors='replace') for c in parts if c)
   222	        except (FileNotFoundError, ProcessLookupError):
   223	            continue  # exited between pgrep and read
   224	        argv0 = Path(cmdline.split(' ', 1)[0]).name if cmdline else ''
   225	        if comm in AGENT_NAMES or argv0 in AGENT_NAMES:
   226	            found.append(dict(pid=pid, comm=comm, cmdline=cmdline))
   227	    return found
   228	
   229	
   230	def log_agent_matches(matches):
   231	    for m in matches:
   232	        print(f"agent match: pid={m['pid']} comm={m['comm']} cmdline={m['cmdline']}", flush=True)
   233	
   234	
   235	def agent_found(log=False):
   236	    matches = agent_matches()
   237	    if matches and log:
   238	        log_agent_matches(matches)
   239	    return bool(matches)
   240	
   241	
   242	def free_ports():
   243	    if os.environ.get('PREFIX') == '/data/data/com.termux/files/usr' and not Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
   244	        listeners = root('/system/bin/ss -H -ltn','ports')
   245	        for line in listeners.splitlines():
   246	            fields = line.split()
   247	            if len(fields) >= 4 and re.search(r':(?:8080|8090|8091)$',fields[3]):
   248	                raise RuntimeError('existing listener on a VIS-1 reserved port')
   249	    for port in (8080, 8090, 8091):
   250	        # Check IPv4 and IPv6 loopback, including non-HTTP listeners.
   251	        for family, host in ((socket.AF_INET, '127.0.0.1'), (socket.AF_INET6, '::1')):
   252	            with socket.socket(family) as s:
   253	                s.settimeout(.3)
   254	                if s.connect_ex((host, port)) == 0:
   255	                    raise RuntimeError(f'port {port} already has a listener')
   256	
   257	
   258	def gate_ok(skin, idle):
   259	    return skin is not None and math.isfinite(skin) and skin <= idle + 1.5
   260	
   261	
   262	def telemetry(bridge, allow_agent=False):
   263	    if (bridge/'abort').exists():
   264	        raise SystemExit('owner aborted wrapper')
   265	    d = json.loads((bridge/'latest.json').read_text())
   266	    if time.monotonic() - d['t'] > 12:
   267	        raise RuntimeError('native telemetry stale')
   268	    if d.get('error'):
   269	        raise RuntimeError('native telemetry failed: ' + d['error'])
   270	    if not 50 <= d['capacity'] <= 80 or d['battery_status'] != 'Discharging':
   271	        raise RuntimeError('battery outside 50-80% Discharging band')
   272	    if d['agent'] and not allow_agent:
   273	        log_agent_matches(d.get('agent_detail', []))
   274	        raise RuntimeError('agent found')
   275	    if d['skin'] is None or d['status'] is None:
   276	        raise RuntimeError('missing VIRTUAL-SKIN or Android status')
   277	    if d['status'] >= 4 or d['bat_c'] >= 45:
   278	        raise RuntimeError('thermal stop (Android CRITICAL or battery >=45 C)')
   279	    return d
   280	
   281	
   282	def gate(bridge, idle, now=time.monotonic, sleep=time.sleep):
   283	    start = now()
   284	    while True:
   285	        d = telemetry(bridge)
   286	        if gate_ok(d['skin'], idle):
   287	            return dict(skin=d['skin'], status=d['status'], waited_s=now()-start, missed=False)
   288	        if now()-start >= 900:
   289	            raise RuntimeError('gate missed: not cool after 15 minutes; no warm-start block')
   290	        sleep(5)
   291	
   292	
   293	def post(path, body=None, timeout=30):
   294	    req = urllib.request.Request('http://127.0.0.1:8091'+path,
   295	        data=None if body is None else json.dumps(body).encode(),
   296	        headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
   297	    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
   298	        return json.load(r)
   299	
   300	
   301	def proc_rows():
   302	    """(pid, ppid, pgid) for every process, from /proc/<n>/stat (comm may contain spaces/parens)."""
   303	    for d in Path('/proc').iterdir():
   304	        if d.name.isdigit():
   305	            try:
   306	                rest = (d/'stat').read_text().rsplit(')', 1)[1].split()
   307	                yield int(d.name), int(rest[1]), int(rest[2])
   308	            except (OSError, IndexError, ValueError):
   309	                pass  # exited mid-scan
   310	
   311	
   312	def server_pid(proc):
   313	    # VIS1-FIX2: /proc/<pid>/task/<pid>/children does not exist on this kernel, so every block
   314	    # timed out here and killed a healthy server. Same rule as before, via ppid/pgid, never by name.
   315	    children = [pid for pid, ppid, pgid in proc_rows() if ppid == proc.pid and pgid == proc.pid]
   316	    if len(children) != 1:
   317	        raise RuntimeError('cannot identify the owned timeout child')
   318	    return children[0]
   319	
   320	
   321	def group_alive(pgid):
   322	    return any(g == pgid for _, _, g in proc_rows())
   323	
   324	
   325	def check_cores(pid):
   326	    # Also check the Android cpuset: affinity alone can hide a background restriction.
   327	    status = Path(f'/proc/{pid}/status').read_text()
   328	    m = re.search(r'^Cpus_allowed_list:\s*(.+)$', status, re.M)
   329	    if not m or m.group(1).strip() != '4-7':
   330	        raise RuntimeError('Termux/server moved off cores 4-7')
   331	    # Each thread inherits the launch mask; inspect every server thread for later changes.
   332	    for p in Path(f'/proc/{pid}/task').glob('*/status'):
   333	        match = re.search(r'^Cpus_allowed_list:\s*(.+)$', p.read_text(), re.M)
   334	        if not match or match.group(1).strip() != '4-7':
   335	            raise RuntimeError('server thread moved off cores 4-7')
   336	
   337	
   338	VIS0_PARSER = None
   339	
   340	def request_record(name, data, start, end, log_delta, block):
   341	    tm = data.get('timings', {})
   342	    if not tm:
   343	        raise RuntimeError('response missing server timings')
   344	    reply = data.get('content')
   345	    if block.startswith('V'):
   346	        reply = data['choices'][0]['message']['content']
   347	    # VIS-0 log parser has no server side effects. Rename its agent-era field explicitly.
   348	    global VIS0_PARSER
   349	    if block.startswith('V') and VIS0_PARSER is None:
   350	        VIS0_PARSER = module('vis1_vis0', 'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis0/vis0.py').parse_server_log_delta
   351	    parsed = VIS0_PARSER(log_delta) if block.startswith('V') else {}
   352	    return dict(input=name, started=start, ended=end, wall_ms=(end-start)*1000, reply=reply,
   353	                timings=tm, prompt_tokens=tm.get('prompt_n'), prompt_ms=tm.get('prompt_ms'),
   354	                generated_tokens=tm.get('predicted_n'), predicted_per_second=tm.get('predicted_per_second'),
   355	                draft_n=tm.get('draft_n'), draft_n_accepted=tm.get('draft_n_accepted'),
   356	                image_encode_ms=parsed.get('image_encode_ms_not_a_measurement'),
   357	                image_tokens=parsed.get('image_tokens_logged'), usage=data.get('usage'),
   358	                budget=None if block.startswith('T') else (70 if block in ('V1','V2') else 140),
   359	                mmproj=None if block.startswith('T') else ('BF16' if block in ('V1','V4') else 'Q8_0'))
   360	
   361	
   362	def run_block(block, out, bridge, idle, payloads, t2_draft):
   363	    record = dict(phase_start=time.monotonic(), block=block, label='NOT VALID', reason='interrupted', requests=[], samples=[],
   364	                  gate=None, command=command(block,t2_draft), started_epoch=time.time())
   365	    attempt = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ')
   366	    path = out/f'{block}_{attempt}.json'
   367	    logpath = out/f'{block}_{attempt}.server.log'
   368	    proc = pid = None
   369	    try:
   370	        record['gate'] = gate(bridge,idle)
   371	        free_ports()
   372	        if agent_found(log=True):
   373	            raise RuntimeError('agent found before launch')
   374	        # Cold load handshake, served only by native owner wrapper.
   375	        (bridge/'drop_done').unlink(missing_ok=True)
   376	        (bridge/'drop_request').touch()
   377	        began = time.monotonic()
   378	        while not (bridge/'drop_done').exists():
   379	            if time.monotonic()-began > 30:
   380	                raise RuntimeError('cache-drop handshake timeout')
   381	            time.sleep(.1)
   382	        if (bridge/'drop_done').read_text().strip() != 'ok':
   383	            raise RuntimeError('cache drop failed')
   384	        (bridge/'drop_request').unlink()
   385	        (bridge/'drop_done').unlink()
   386	        # Cache drop can take time: the actual launch still must satisfy the gate.
   387	        record['gate_after_drop'] = gate(bridge,idle)
   388	        with logpath.open('w') as log:
   389	            began = time.monotonic()
   390	            proc = spawn(record['command'],log)
   391	            pid = None
   392	            while pid is None:
   393	                if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic()-began > 10:
   394	                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed or owned child not found')
   395	                try:
   396	                    pid = server_pid(proc)
   397	                except (OSError,RuntimeError):
   398	                    time.sleep(.05)
   399	            write(bridge/'target.json',dict(pid=pid))
   400	            while True:
   401	                if proc.poll() is not None:
   402	                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed during load')
   403	                try:
   404	                    if post('/health',timeout=1).get('status') == 'ok':
   405	                        break
   406	                except (OSError, ValueError):
   407	                    pass
   408	                telemetry(bridge)
   409	                if time.monotonic()-began >= 180:
   410	                    raise RuntimeError('server load timeout')
   411	                time.sleep(.2)
   412	            pid = server_pid(proc)
   413	            write(bridge/'target.json',dict(pid=pid))
   414	            record.update(server_pid=pid,load_s=time.monotonic()-began)
   415	            check_cores(pid)
   416	            endpoint = '/completion' if block.startswith('T') else '/v1/chat/completions'
   417	            post(endpoint,payloads[0][1],timeout=180)  # discarded warm-up
   418	            check_cores(pid)
   419	            telemetry(bridge)
   420	            record['measure_start'] = start = time.monotonic()
   421	            deadline = start+BLOCK_S
   422	            k = 0
   423	            while time.monotonic() < deadline:
   424	                telemetry(bridge)
   425	                check_cores(pid)
   426	                name, body = payloads[k % len(payloads)]
   427	                offset = logpath.stat().st_size
   428	                t = time.monotonic()
   429	                try:
   430	                    data = post(endpoint,body,timeout=max(.001,deadline-t))
   431	                except (TimeoutError, OSError) as e:
   432	                    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
   433	                        record['cutoff_request'] = dict(input=name,reason='180 s boundary; excluded',error=type(e).__name__)
   434	                        break
   435	                    raise
   436	                end = time.monotonic()
   437	                if end > deadline:
   438	                    record['cutoff_request'] = dict(input=name,reason='reply after boundary; excluded')
   439	                    break
   440	                with logpath.open() as f:
   441	                    f.seek(offset)
   442	                    delta = f.read()
   443	                row = request_record(name,data,t,end,delta,block)
   444	                record['requests'].append(row)
   445	                write(path,record)  # keep replies across interruption
   446	                k += 1
   447	            record['measure_end'] = deadline
   448	            if proc.poll() is not None:
   449	                raise RuntimeError('server crashed during measurement')
   450	            check_cores(pid)
   451	            telemetry(bridge)
   452	            record.update(label='VALID',reason='completed 180 s request window')
   453	    except (Exception, SystemExit) as e:
   454	        record.update(label='NOT VALID',reason=str(e))
   455	    finally:
   456	        record['stop_start'] = time.monotonic()
   457	        write(bridge/'target.json',dict(pid=None))
   458	        if proc is not None:
   459	            record['server_exit'] = own_stop(proc, pid)
   460	        record['stopped'] = time.monotonic()
   461	        record['samples'] = [s for s in rows(bridge/'samples.jsonl')
   462	                             if s['t'] >= record['phase_start']-1
   463	                             and s['t'] <= record['stopped']]
   464	        record['lmk'] = lmk_request(bridge,record['started_epoch'])
   465	        if not record['lmk'].get('ok'):
   466	            record.update(label='NOT VALID',reason=record['reason']+'; LMK query failed')
   467	        if not record['requests']:
   468	            record.update(label='NOT VALID',reason=record['reason']+'; no completed requests')
   469	        record['metrics'] = metrics(record)
   470	        if any(s.get('agent') or s.get('battery_status') != 'Discharging' or not 50 <= s.get('capacity',-1) <= 80
   471	               or s.get('cores_ok') is False for s in record['samples']):
   472	            record.update(label='NOT VALID',reason=record['reason']+'; agent, battery or affinity violation in samples')
   473	        errors = [e for e in rows(bridge/'errors.jsonl') if record['phase_start'] <= e['t'] <= record['stop_start']]
   474	        record['sampling_errors'] = errors
   475	        if errors:
   476	            record.update(label='NOT VALID',reason=record['reason']+'; sampling failed')
   477	        write(path,record)
   478	    return record
   479	
   480	
   481	def metrics(record):
   482	    # Reuse the campaign's receipt-time trapezoids; no extrapolation across missing samples.
   483	    sys.path.insert(0,str(REPO/'benchmark/campaign'))
   484	    import power
   485	    samples = record['samples']
   486	    start,end = record.get('measure_start'),record.get('measure_end')
   487	    measured = [s for s in samples if start is not None and start <= s['t'] <= (end or start)]
   488	    result = dict(peak_vmhwm_kib=max((s['vmhwm_kib'] for s in samples if s.get('vmhwm_kib') is not None),default=None),
   489	                  peak_pss_kib=max((s['pss_kib'] for s in samples if s.get('pss_kib') is not None),default=None),
   490	                  min_mem_available_kib=min((s['mem_available_kib'] for s in samples),default=None))
   491	    if start is None or end is None:
   492	        return result
   493	    result['power'] = power.weighted(samples,start,end)
   494	    result['skin_start'] = measured[0]['skin'] if measured else None
   495	    result['skin_end'] = measured[-1]['skin'] if measured else None
   496	    result['status_max'] = max((s['status'] for s in measured if s.get('status') is not None),default=None)
   497	    # Marked copy from power_map.capped_by_policy: later sample owns the previous gap.
   498	    result['caps'] = {}
   499	    for policy in POLICIES:
   500	        previous,capped,known = start,0.,0.
   501	        for s in measured:
   502	            dt = s['t']-previous
   503	            previous = s['t']
   504	            value,top = s['max'].get(policy),s['cpuinfo_max_khz'].get(policy)
   505	            if value is not None and top is not None:
   506	                known += dt
   507	                if value < top:
   508	                    capped += dt
   509	        result['caps'][policy] = dict(percent=100*capped/(end-start),unknown_s=(end-start)-known)
   510	    return result
   511	
   512	
   513	def lmk_request(bridge, epoch):
   514	    write(bridge/'lmk_request.json',dict(epoch=epoch))
   515	    start = time.monotonic()
   516	    while not (bridge/'lmk_done.json').exists():
   517	        if time.monotonic()-start > 40:
   518	            return dict(ok=False,error='LMK bridge timeout')
   519	        time.sleep(.1)
   520	    d = json.loads((bridge/'lmk_done.json').read_text())
   521	    (bridge/'lmk_request.json').unlink()
   522	    (bridge/'lmk_done.json').unlink()
   523	    return d
   524	
   525	
   526	def root(script, tag):
   527	    """#123 form, native only: Android-service output goes to a file before it is read."""
   528	    dest = f'/data/local/tmp/vis1_{os.getpid()}_{tag}.txt'
   529	    p = subprocess.run(['su','-c',f'({script}) </dev/null >{dest} 2>&1'],
   530	                       capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=30)
   531	    q = subprocess.run(['su','-c',f'cat {dest}'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=10)
   532	    if p.returncode or q.returncode:
   533	        raise RuntimeError(f'root {tag} failed ({p.returncode}/{q.returncode})')
   534	    return q.stdout
   535	
   536	
   537	def parse_dump(text):
   538	    # Marked copy from coresidency.parse_dump: HAL current section only, never cached temperatures.
   539	    status = re.search(r'^Thermal Status: (\d+)\s*$',text,re.M)
   540	    section = re.search(r'^Current temperatures from HAL:\n((?:[ \t]+[^\n]*\n?)*)',text,re.M)
   541	    skin = re.search(r'Temperature\{mValue=([^,]+), mType=-?\d+, mName=VIRTUAL-SKIN,',
   542	                     section.group(1) if section else '')
   543	    return dict(status=int(status.group(1)) if status else None,skin=float(skin.group(1)) if skin else None)
   544	
   545	
   546	def monitor(bridge):
   547	    if Path('/termux-home').is_dir() or os.environ.get('PREFIX') != '/data/data/com.termux/files/usr':
   548	        raise SystemExit('monitor must run in native Termux')
   549	    os.sched_setaffinity(0,{0,1,2,3})
   550	    slow_state, ready = {}, threading.Event()
   551	    def slow_samples():
   552	        while not (bridge/'stop').exists():
   553	            began = time.monotonic()
   554	            try:
   555	                matches = agent_matches()
   556	                slow_state['latest'] = dict(parse_dump(root('dumpsys thermalservice','thermal')),
   557	                                            thermal_at=time.monotonic(),agent=bool(matches),agent_detail=matches)
   558	            except Exception as e:
   559	                slow_state['latest'] = dict(error=str(e))
   560	                append(bridge/'errors.jsonl',dict(t=time.monotonic(),error=str(e)))
   561	            ready.set()
   562	            # Slow HAL calls must not create holes in campaign-style power integration.
   563	            for _ in range(max(0,int((5-(time.monotonic()-began))/.1))):
   564	                if (bridge/'stop').exists():return
   565	                time.sleep(.1)
   566	    slow_thread = threading.Thread(target=slow_samples,daemon=True)
   567	    slow_thread.start()
   568	    if not ready.wait(35):
   569	        raise RuntimeError('native thermal monitor did not initialize')
   570	    with (bridge/'samples.jsonl').open('a') as log:
   571	        while not (bridge/'stop').exists():
   572	            began = time.monotonic()
   573	            try:
   574	                latest = dict(slow_state['latest'])
   575	                if latest.get('error'):
   576	                    raise RuntimeError(latest['error'])
   577	                pid = json.loads((bridge/'target.json').read_text()).get('pid') if (bridge/'target.json').exists() else None
   578	                script = f'cat {BAT}/current_now {BAT}/voltage_now {BAT}/status {BAT}/capacity {BAT}/temp; '
   579	                script += 'echo CAPS; '
   580	                script += '; '.join(f'cat {CPU}/{p}/scaling_max_freq {CPU}/{p}/cpuinfo_max_freq' for p in POLICIES)
   581	                script += '; echo CPU; cat /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone9/temp /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone10/temp /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone11/temp'
   582	                if pid:
   583	                    script += f'; echo MEMORY; cat /proc/{pid}/status; echo PSS; cat /proc/{pid}/smaps_rollup'
   584	                text = root(script,'sample')
   585	                head, text = text.split('CAPS\n',1)
   586	                current,voltage,status,capacity,bat_c = head.split()
   587	                caps,text = text.split('CPU\n',1)
   588	                nums = list(map(int,caps.split()))
   589	                if len(nums) != 6 or int(voltage) <= 0:
   590	                    raise RuntimeError('invalid power/cap fields')
   591	                cpu = text.split('MEMORY\n')[0].split()
   592	                row = dict(latest,t_start=began,t=time.monotonic(),current_now_uA=int(current),voltage_now_uV=int(voltage),
   593	                           battery_w=-int(current)*int(voltage)/1e12,battery_status=status,capacity=int(capacity),bat_c=int(bat_c)/10,
   594	                           max=dict(zip(POLICIES,nums[::2])),cpuinfo_max_khz=dict(zip(POLICIES,nums[1::2])),
   595	                           cpu_mc=list(map(int,cpu[:3])),pid=pid)
   596	                mem = Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text()
   597	                row['mem_available_kib'] = int(re.search(r'MemAvailable:\s*(\d+)',mem).group(1))
   598	                if pid:
   599	                    for key,pat in [('vmhwm_kib',r'^VmHWM:\s*(\d+)'),('pss_kib',r'^Pss:\s*(\d+)')]:
   600	                        m = re.search(pat,text,re.M)
   601	                        row[key] = int(m.group(1)) if m else None
   602	                    masks = re.findall(r'^Cpus_allowed_list:\s*(.+)$',text,re.M)
   603	                    row['cores_ok'] = masks == ['4-7']
   604	                if time.monotonic()-row['thermal_at'] > 12:
   605	                    raise RuntimeError('thermal sample stale')
   606	                write(bridge/'latest.json',row)
   607	                log.write(json.dumps(row)+'\n');log.flush()
   608	                if (bridge/'lmk_request.json').exists() and not (bridge/'lmk_done.json').exists():
   609	                    epoch = float(json.loads((bridge/'lmk_request.json').read_text())['epoch'])
   610	                    # Marked copy: coresidency.lmk_lines patterns, with no raw logcat retained (privacy).
   611	                    # Filter before root output hits disk: never save unrelated Android logs or identities.
   612	                    awk = "BEGIN {n=0;k=0} {s=tolower($0)} s ~ /lowmemorykiller|lmkd|killinfo/ {n++; if (s ~ /(lowmemorykiller|lmkd).*kill|killinfo/) k++} END {print n,k}"
   613	                    counts = root(f"set -o pipefail; logcat -d -b all -v epoch -T {epoch:.3f} | awk '{awk}'",'lmk')
   614	                    n,k = map(int,counts.split())
   615	                    write(bridge/'lmk_done.json',dict(ok=True,n_lines=n,n_kills=k))
   616	            except Exception as e:
   617	                write(bridge/'latest.json',dict(t=time.monotonic(),error=str(e)))
   618	                append(bridge/'errors.jsonl',dict(t=time.monotonic(),error=str(e)))
   619	            # Campaign de-phased receipt-time power sampling, same 0.37 s period, no catch-up bursts.
   620	            time.sleep(max(0,.37-(time.monotonic()-began)))
   621	
   622	
   623	def manifest(selected, t2_draft, texts, images):
   624	    paths = ['chat.py','server_manager.py','benchmark/llm_objective_setting/bench.py',
   625	             'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis0/vis0.py','benchmark/campaign/power.py',
   626	             'benchmark/campaign/runtime.py','benchmark/gemma_vision/vis1/vis1_run.py',
   627	             'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis1/oneshot.sh']
   628	    return dict(blocks=selected,block_s=BLOCK_S,t2_draft=t2_draft,
   629	                commands={b:command(b,t2_draft) for b in selected},
   630	                payload_sha256={n:hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(p,sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest() for n,p in texts+images},
   631	                code_sha256={p:sha(REPO/p) for p in paths})
   632	
   633	
   634	def completed(out):
   635	    return {d['block'] for p in out.glob('[TV][1-4]_*.json')
   636	            if (d:=json.loads(p.read_text())).get('label') == 'VALID'}
   637	
   638	
   639	def preflight():
   640	    """Everything oneshot.sh needs true before it idles 5 minutes: ports, files/hashes, agent, plus
   641	    whichever of (Debian imports) / (root, battery, cpuset) matches where this process is running."""
   642	    free_ports()
   643	    matches = agent_matches()
   644	    if matches:
   645	        log_agent_matches(matches)
   646	        raise SystemExit('agent found; close Claude/AGY/Codex (see pid/comm/cmdline above)')
   647	    missing = [str(p) for p in (*EXPECTED_HASHES, *BINS.values()) if not p.is_file()]
   648	    if missing:
   649	        raise SystemExit('missing file(s), fetch/build first: ' + ', '.join(missing))
   650	    bad = [str(p) for p, h in EXPECTED_HASHES.items() if sha(p) != h]
   651	    if bad:
   652	        raise SystemExit('SHA-256 mismatch, re-fetch/rebuild: ' + ', '.join(bad))
   653	    if Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
   654	        missing_mods = [m for m in DEBIAN_IMPORTS if importlib.util.find_spec(m) is None]
   655	        if missing_mods:
   656	            raise SystemExit('Debian python3 is missing ' + ', '.join(missing_mods) +
   657	                              ' — run: apt-get install python3-requests python3-pil')
   658	    else:
   659	        if subprocess.run(['su', '-c', 'id'], capture_output=True, timeout=10).returncode != 0:
   660	            raise SystemExit('no root (su) access; telemetry needs root')
   661	        cap, status = root('cat /sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'
   662	                            ' /sys/class/power_supply/battery/status', 'preflight_battery').split()[:2]
   663	        if not (cap.isdigit() and 50 <= int(cap) <= 80 and status == 'Discharging'):
   664	            raise SystemExit('battery outside 50-80% Discharging band; unplug and bring it into range')
   665	        before = os.sched_getaffinity(0)
   666	        os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
   667	        stuck = os.sched_getaffinity(0) == {4, 5, 6, 7}
   668	        os.sched_setaffinity(0, before)
   669	        if not stuck:
   670	            raise SystemExit('Termux cpuset blocks cores 4-7; bring Termux to the foreground, screen on')
   671	
   672	
   673	def smoke_block(block, out, bridge, t2_draft, allow_agent, payloads):
   674	    """--smoke: one request per block, own server up and down, no 180 s loop or gate wait beyond
   675	    the single telemetry reading already needed to launch. Always labelled SMOKE — NOT VALID TIMING."""
   676	    record = dict(block=block, label=SMOKE_LABEL, command=command(block, t2_draft),
   677	                  started_epoch=time.time(), server_started=False, reply_received=False,
   678	                  exit_clean=None, image_tokens=None, reason=None, allow_agent=allow_agent)
   679	    attempt = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ')
   680	    logpath = out / f'{block}_{attempt}.server.log'
   681	    proc = pid = None
   682	    try:
   683	        record['reading'] = telemetry(bridge, allow_agent=allow_agent)
   684	        free_ports()
   685	        if not allow_agent:
   686	            matches = agent_matches()
   687	            if matches:
   688	                log_agent_matches(matches)
   689	                raise RuntimeError('agent found before launch')
   690	        with logpath.open('w') as log:
   691	            began = time.monotonic()
   692	            proc = spawn(record['command'], log)
   693	            pid = None
   694	            while pid is None:
   695	                if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() - began > 10:
   696	                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed or owned child not found')
   697	                try:
   698	                    pid = server_pid(proc)
   699	                except (OSError, RuntimeError):
   700	                    time.sleep(.05)
   701	            record['server_started'] = True
   702	            write(bridge / 'target.json', dict(pid=pid))
   703	            while True:
   704	                if proc.poll() is not None:
   705	                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed during load')
   706	                try:
   707	                    if post('/health', timeout=1).get('status') == 'ok':
   708	                        break
   709	                except (OSError, ValueError):
   710	                    pass
   711	                if time.monotonic() - began >= 180:
   712	                    raise RuntimeError('server load timeout')
   713	                time.sleep(.2)
   714	            check_cores(pid)
   715	            endpoint = '/completion' if block.startswith('T') else '/v1/chat/completions'
   716	            name, body = payloads[0]
   717	            offset = logpath.stat().st_size
   718	            t0 = time.monotonic()
   719	            data = post(endpoint, body, timeout=180)  # same bound as the real warm-up; V4 (BF16) took 55-72 s
   720	            end = time.monotonic()
   721	            with logpath.open() as f:
   722	                f.seek(offset)
   723	                delta = f.read()
   724	            row = request_record(name, data, t0, end, delta, block)
   725	            record['reply_received'] = True
   726	            record['image_tokens'] = row.get('image_tokens')
   727	            record['requests'] = [row]
   728	    except Exception as e:
   729	        record['reason'] = str(e)
   730	    finally:
   731	        write(bridge / 'target.json', dict(pid=None))
   732	        if proc is not None:
   733	            # One TERM to the server lets it exit 0; clean = exit 0 and nothing left in our group.
   734	            record['server_exit'] = own_stop(proc, pid)
   735	            record['exit_clean'] = record['server_exit'] == 0 and not group_alive(proc.pid)
   736	        write(out / f'{block}_{attempt}.json', record)
   737	    return record
   738	
   739	
   740	def main(argv=None):
   741	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
   742	    ap.add_argument('--only')
   743	    ap.add_argument('--resume',type=Path)
   744	    ap.add_argument('--dry-run',action='store_true')
   745	    ap.add_argument('--bridge',type=Path)
   746	    ap.add_argument('--preflight',action='store_true',help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
   747	    ap.add_argument('--monitor',type=Path,help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
   748	    ap.add_argument('--smoke',action='store_true',help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
   749	    ap.add_argument('--allow-agent',action='store_true',help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
   750	    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
   751	    if a.allow_agent and not a.smoke:
   752	        raise SystemExit('--allow-agent is only valid with --smoke; the real run never accepts it')
   753	    if a.preflight:
   754	        preflight()
   755	        return
   756	    if a.monitor:
   757	        return monitor(a.monitor)
   758	    selected = blocks(a.only)
   759	    if a.dry_run:
   760	        print(DRY_LABEL)
   761	        print(json.dumps(dict(block_order=selected,commands={b:command(b) for b in selected},
   762	                             text_example=text_payload('Hi'),vision_example=vision_payload(b'offline-fake-jpeg','- chair [0], center, near')),
   763	                         indent=2))
   764	        return
   765	    if a.bridge is None or not Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
   766	        raise SystemExit('launch only through native oneshot.sh into Debian')
   767	    bridge = a.bridge.resolve()
   768	    if not (bridge/'owner_launch').is_file():
   769	        raise SystemExit('missing owner wrapper receipt')
   770	    if not (a.smoke and a.allow_agent) and agent_found(log=True):
   771	        raise SystemExit('agent found; no server started')
   772	    free_ports()
   773	    telemetry(bridge, allow_agent=a.smoke and a.allow_agent)
   774	    os.sched_setaffinity(0,{4,5,6,7})
   775	    check_cores(os.getpid())
   776	    for sig in (signal.SIGINT,signal.SIGTERM,signal.SIGHUP):
   777	        signal.signal(sig,interrupted)
   778	    # Help is inspected only by the owner runner; no server/model is loaded by the preparer.
   779	    help_result = subprocess.run(['timeout','10',str(BINS['vis']),'--help'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=15)
   780	    if help_result.returncode:
   781	        raise SystemExit('b11716 --help failed')
   782	    help_text = help_result.stdout+help_result.stderr
   783	    t2_draft = all(s in help_text for s in ('--model-draft','--spec-type','--spec-draft-n-max','draft-mtp'))
   784	    texts,images = inputs()
   785	    if a.smoke:
   786	        out = HERE/'runs'/('smoke_'+datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ'))
   787	        out.mkdir(parents=True)
   788	        write(out/'smoke.json',dict(label=SMOKE_LABEL,allow_agent=a.allow_agent,t2_draft=t2_draft,
   789	             base_commit=subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=REPO,text=True).strip()))
   790	        print('RUN_FOLDER='+str(out),flush=True)
   791	        for b in selected:
   792	            result = smoke_block(b,out,bridge,t2_draft,a.allow_agent,texts if b.startswith('T') else images)
   793	            print(b+': SMOKE server_started='+str(result['server_started'])+
   794	                  ' reply_received='+str(result['reply_received'])+' image_tokens='+str(result['image_tokens'])+
   795	                  ' exit_clean='+str(result['exit_clean'])+
   796	                  ('' if not result['reason'] else ' reason='+result['reason']),flush=True)
   797	        print('Smoke finished ('+SMOKE_LABEL+'). Send the RUN_FOLDER path and console log.',flush=True)
   798	        return
   799	    expected = EXPECTED_HASHES
   800	    hashes = {str(p):sha(p) for p in expected}
   801	    if any(hashes[str(p)] != h for p,h in expected.items()):
   802	        raise SystemExit('model/projector/drafter SHA-256 mismatch')
   803	    spec = manifest(selected,t2_draft,texts,images)
   804	    if a.resume:
   805	        out = a.resume.resolve()
   806	        if not out.is_relative_to(HERE/'runs'):
   807	            raise SystemExit('resume folder must be under vis1/runs')
   808	        prior = json.loads((out/'run.json').read_text())
   809	        if prior['manifest'] != spec:
   810	            raise SystemExit('resume manifest mismatch (code, payloads, flags or block selection)')
   811	        idle = prior['idle_skin']
   812	    else:
   813	        out = HERE/'runs'/datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ')
   814	        out.mkdir(parents=True)
   815	        idle = json.loads((bridge/'idle.json').read_text())['skin']
   816	        write(out/'run.json',dict(manifest=spec,idle_skin=idle,weight_sha256=hashes,
   817	             base_commit=subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=REPO,text=True).strip(),
   818	             binaries={str(p):sha(p) for p in BINS.values()},
   819	             note='180 s short blocks; sustained thermal equilibrium is not established (#93). T1 reference #137: 19.18 tok/s, 39.0% acceptance; pre TPL-FIX.'))
   820	    print('RUN_FOLDER='+str(out),flush=True)
   821	    print('T2 drafter: '+('enabled' if t2_draft else 'not supported by b11716 help; running without'),flush=True)
   822	    for b in selected:
   823	        if b in completed(out):
   824	            print('resume skips '+b,flush=True)
   825	            continue
   826	        result = run_block(b,out,bridge,idle,texts if b.startswith('T') else images,t2_draft)
   827	        print(b+': '+result['label']+' — '+result['reason'],flush=True)
   828	        if result['label'] != 'VALID':
   829	            raise SystemExit('stopped; inspect block record before --resume')
   830	    print('Finished. Send the RUN_FOLDER path and console log; do not rerun for Phase 2.',flush=True)
   831	
   832	
   833	if __name__ == '__main__':
   834	    try:
   835	        main()
   836	    finally:
   837	        for proc in OWNED:
   838	            own_stop(proc)

     1	"""Receipt-time power integration and de-phased, pinned root sampling."""
     2	import math
     3	import os
     4	import threading
     5	import time
     6	
     7	import runtime as rt
     8	
     9	PERIOD = .37
    10	
    11	
    12	def safe_mask(allowed, measured):
    13	    mask = set(allowed) - set(measured)
    14	    if not mask or mask & set(measured):
    15	        raise RuntimeError('empty/unsafe monitor CPU mask')
    16	    return mask
    17	
    18	
    19	def sample(shell, battery, t0, average=False):
    20	    fields = ['current_now', 'voltage_now', 'status']
    21	    script = 'for f in ' + ' '.join(battery+'/'+f for f in fields)
    22	    script += '; do v=; read -r v <"$f"; printf "%s\\n" "$v"; done'
    23	    if average:
    24	        script += f'; if [ -e {battery}/current_avg ]; then v=; read -r v <{battery}/current_avg; echo "$v"; else echo ABSENT; fi'
    25	    def read():
    26	        start = time.monotonic() - t0
    27	        values = shell.run(script)
    28	        receipt = time.monotonic() - t0
    29	        if rt.shell_nothing(shell, values):  # no stdout and no su stderr: the only re-read (H2, FIX4C/FIX4D)
    30	            raise rt.Blip('empty power answer: '+repr(values))
    31	        # Base parser and checks (5629699), unchanged.
    32	        if len(values) != 3 + int(average) or values[2] != 'Discharging':
    33	            raise RuntimeError('power read missing/charger connected: '+repr(values))
    34	        current, voltage = map(int, values[:2])
    35	        if voltage <= 0:
    36	            raise RuntimeError('invalid battery voltage')
    37	        row = dict(t_start=start, t=receipt, current_now_uA=current, voltage_now_uV=voltage,
    38	                   battery_w=-current*voltage/1e12, battery_status=values[2])
    39	        if average:
    40	            row['current_avg_uA'] = None if values[3] == 'ABSENT' else int(values[3])
    41	        return row
    42	    return rt.reread('power', read)
    43	
    44	
    45	def weighted(rows, lo, hi, max_gap=1.5):
    46	    """Trapezoids clipped/interpolated to boundaries; never extrapolate a missing tail."""
    47	    if hi <= lo:
    48	        return dict(mean_battery_w=None, covered_s=0., samples=0, gaps=0)
    49	    points = sorted((r['t'], r['battery_w']) for r in rows if 'battery_w' in r)
    50	    energy = covered = 0.
    51	    gaps = 0
    52	    for (a, wa), (b, wb) in zip(points, points[1:]):
    53	        l, h = max(lo, a), min(hi, b)
    54	        if h <= l:
    55	            continue
    56	        if b-a > max_gap:
    57	            gaps += 1
    58	            continue
    59	        wl, wh = wa+(wb-wa)*(l-a)/(b-a), wa+(wb-wa)*(h-a)/(b-a)
    60	        energy += (wl+wh)*.5*(h-l)
    61	        covered += h-l
    62	    complete = abs(covered-(hi-lo)) < 1e-6 and not gaps
    63	    return dict(mean_battery_w=energy/(hi-lo) if complete else None,
    64	                covered_s=covered, samples=sum(lo <= t <= hi for t, _ in points), gaps=gaps)
    65	
    66	
    67	def summary(rows, calls, duration):
    68	    inside = lambda r: any(c['started_s'] <= r['t'] < c['ended_s'] for c in calls)
    69	    block = [r for r in rows if 0 <= r['t'] <= duration]
    70	    return dict(**weighted(rows, 0, duration), inside_samples=sum(inside(r) for r in block),
    71	                outside_samples=sum(not inside(r) for r in block),
    72	                read_overlaps_call_boundary=sum(any(r['t_start'] < c[k] <= r['t']
    73	                    for c in calls for k in ('started_s', 'ended_s')) for r in block),
    74	                period_s=PERIOD, method='trapezoid over receipt times; lag remains a method limitation')
    75	
    76	
    77	def sampler(shell, battery, t0, mask, measured, stop, rows, errors, verify, period=PERIOD, average=False):
    78	    try:
    79	        tid = threading.get_native_id()
    80	        os.sched_setaffinity(tid, mask)
    81	        safe_mask(os.sched_getaffinity(tid), measured)
    82	        if os.sched_getaffinity(tid) != set(mask):
    83	            raise RuntimeError('sampler pinning failed')
    84	        k = 0
    85	        next_verify = -math.inf
    86	        while not stop.is_set():
    87	            now = time.monotonic()
    88	            mark = len(rt.READ_RETRIES)
    89	            if period or now >= next_verify:
    90	                verify()  # cadence mode: each read; fastest probe: once per second
    91	                next_verify = now + 1.
    92	            row = sample(shell, battery, t0, average)
    93	            row['retries'] = rt.retries_since(mark)
    94	            rows.append(row)
    95	            if period:
    96	                k = max(k+1, math.ceil((time.monotonic()-t0)/period))
    97	                if stop.wait(max(0., t0+k*period-time.monotonic())):
    98	                    break
    99	        mark = len(rt.READ_RETRIES)
   100	        verify()
   101	        row = sample(shell, battery, t0, average)  # end bracket, same reader owns shell
   102	        row['retries'] = rt.retries_since(mark)
   103	        rows.append(row)
   104	    except BaseException as e:
   105	        errors.append(f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}')
   106	        stop.set()
   107	
   108	
   109	def lag_response(rows, load_start, load_end, key='current_now_uA'):
   110	    """Baseline/plateau from final 5 s of quiet/load; crossing interpolation, absent => null."""
   111	    # Discharging current is negative; convert to positive load magnitude.
   112	    points = sorted((r['t'], -r[key]) for r in rows if r.get(key) is not None)
   113	    def mean(lo, hi):
   114	        xs = [v for t, v in points if lo <= t < hi]
   115	        return sum(xs)/len(xs) if xs else None
   116	    baseline = mean(load_start-5, load_start)
   117	    plateau = mean(max(load_start, load_end-5), load_end)
   118	    result = dict(baseline_uA=baseline, plateau_uA=plateau, step_uA=None,
   119	                  rise_50_s=None, rise_90_s=None, fall_50_s=None, fall_10_s=None)
   120	    if baseline is None or plateau is None or plateau <= baseline:
   121	        result['unresolved'] = 'no positive observed step; probe cannot identify lag'
   122	        return result
   123	    result['step_uA'] = step = plateau-baseline
   124	    def crossing(at, end, fraction, rising):
   125	        target = baseline+fraction*step
   126	        candidates = [(t, v) for t, v in points if at <= t <= end]
   127	        if candidates and (candidates[0][1] >= target if rising else candidates[0][1] <= target):
   128	            return candidates[0][0]-at
   129	        for (a, va), (b, vb) in zip(candidates, candidates[1:]):
   130	            if (va < target <= vb) if rising else (va > target >= vb):
   131	                return a+(target-va)*(b-a)/(vb-va)-at
   132	        return None
   133	    for pct in (50, 90):
   134	        result[f'rise_{pct}_s'] = crossing(load_start, load_end, pct/100, True)
   135	    for pct in (50, 10):
   136	        result[f'fall_{pct}_s'] = crossing(load_end, points[-1][0], pct/100, False)
   137	    result['estimator'] = 'observed last-5-s plateau, not a fitted physical delay; unresolved crossings are null'
   138	    return result

    79	+        cmd += ['--mmproj', str(v1.Q8), '--image-min-tokens', str(IMAGE_TOKENS), '--image-max-tokens', str(IMAGE_TOKENS)]
    80	+    return cmd
    81	+
    82	+
    83	+def files_of(cfg):
    84	+    """Everything the server reads at load: weights it uses plus its build's libraries."""
    85	+    return ([v1.MODEL] + ([v1.DRAFT] if cfg['mtp'] else []) + ([v1.Q8] if cfg['q8'] else [])
    86	+            + [v1.BINS[BUILDS[cfg['build']]]] + libs_of(BUILDS[cfg['build']]))
    87	+
    88	+
    89	+def libs_of(build):
    90	+    return sorted(v1.BINS[build].parent.glob('lib*.so*'))
    91	+
    92	+
    93	+def payloads(cfg):
    94	+    """(warm-up, [(kind, name, endpoint, body)]) for the test; load tests send nothing."""
    95	+    gen, unc, cached = v2.payloads()
    96	+    first = ('F_' + gen[0][0], dict(gen[0][1], n_predict=FIRST_N))
    97	+    test = cfg['test']
    98	+    if test in LOAD_TESTS:
    99	+        return None, []
   100	+    if test == 'vision':
   101	+        images = dict(v1.inputs()[1])
   102	+        return (('image', WARMUP_FRAME, '/v1/chat/completions', images[WARMUP_FRAME]),
   103	+                [('image', f, '/v1/chat/completions', images[f]) for f in VISION_FRAMES])
   104	+    warm = ('first', *first[:1], '/completion', first[1])
   105	+    if test == 'first_response':
   106	+        return None, [warm]
   107	+    if test == 'prompt':
   108	+        seq = []
   109	+        for k in range(ROUNDS):
   110	+            seq += [('uncached', *unc), ('cached', *cached[k])]
   111	+        return warm, [(kind, name, '/completion', body) for kind, name, body in seq]
   112	+    return warm, [('gen', name, '/completion', body) for name, body in gen]
   113	+
   114	+
   115	+def stamp_s(m):
   116	+    mi, se, ms, us = map(int, m.groups())
   117	+    return round(mi*60 + se + ms/1e3 + us/1e6, 3)
   118	+
   119	+
   120	+def log_markers(text):
   121	+    out = {}
   122	+    for line in text.splitlines():
   123	+        m = STAMP.match(line)
   124	+        if m:
   125	+            for key, needle in MARKERS.items():
   126	+                if key not in out and needle in line:
   127	+                    out[key] = stamp_s(m)
   128	+    return out
   129	+
   130	+
   131	+def freq_state(root=CPUFREQ):
   132	+    """cpufreq time_in_state (10 ms ticks per frequency); readable without root."""
   133	+    state = {}
   134	+    for p in POLICIES:
   135	+        rows = (line.split() for line in (root/p/'stats/time_in_state').read_text().splitlines() if line.strip())
   136	+        state[p] = {int(f): int(t) for f, t in rows}
   137	+    return state
   138	+
   139	+
   140	+def freq_delta(a, b):
   141	+    """Mean policy frequency (MHz) and share of time at its top frequency between two snapshots."""
   142	+    out = {}
   143	+    for p in POLICIES:
   144	+        d = {f: b[p][f] - a[p].get(f, 0) for f in b[p]}
   145	+        total = sum(d.values())
   146	+        out[p] = dict(ticks=total, mean_mhz=round(sum(f*t for f, t in d.items())/total/1000) if total > 0 else None,
   147	+                      top_share=round(d[max(d)]/total, 3) if total > 0 else None)
   148	+    return out
   149	+
   150	+
   151	+def mapped_libs(pid, build, maps=None):
   152	+    """llama/ggml/mtmd libraries the running server actually mapped; all must come from its own build."""
   153	+    text = maps if maps is not None else Path(f'/proc/{pid}/maps').read_text()
   154	+    libs = sorted({m.group(1) for line in text.splitlines() if (m := LIB.search(line))})
   155	+    want = str(v1.BINS[build].parent) + '/'
   156	+    return dict(libs=libs, ok=bool(libs) and all(x.startswith(want) for x in libs))
   157	+
   158	+
   159	+def verify(cfg):
   160	+    """The running server must report the intended projector and MTP state and ctx 2048; never adjust flags."""
   161	+    props, slots = v2.get('/props'), v2.get('/slots')
   162	+    got = dict(vision=props.get('modalities', {}).get('vision'), speculative=[s.get('speculative') for s in slots],
   163	+               n_ctx=[s.get('n_ctx') for s in slots], build_info=props.get('build_info'))
   164	+    if got['vision'] is not cfg['q8'] or got['speculative'] != [cfg['mtp']] or got['n_ctx'] != [2048]:
   165	+        raise RuntimeError(f"configuration mismatch: expected vision={cfg['q8']} mtp={cfg['mtp']} n_ctx=2048, "
   166	+                           f'server reports {got}')
   167	+    return got
   168	+
   169	+
   170	+def snapshot(bridge):
   171	+    """Operating conditions now: VIS-2 telemetry (with all its stops) plus cpufreq residency counters."""
   172	+    d = v2.telemetry(bridge)
   173	+    keep = ('skin', 'status', 'bat_c', 'capacity', 'voltage_now_uV', 'battery_w', 'mem_available_kib', 'max',
   174	+            'cpuinfo_max_khz', 'cpu_mc', 't')
   175	+    return dict({k: d.get(k) for k in keep}, freq=freq_state())
   176	+
   177	+
   178	+def drop_caches(bridge):
   179	+    """Same native cache-drop handshake as VIS-1/VIS-2."""
   180	+    (bridge/'drop_done').unlink(missing_ok=True)
   181	+    (bridge/'drop_request').touch()
   182	+    began = time.monotonic()
   183	+    while not (bridge/'drop_done').exists():
   184	+        if time.monotonic()-began > 30:
   185	+            raise RuntimeError('cache-drop handshake timeout')
   186	+        time.sleep(.1)
   187	+    if (bridge/'drop_done').read_text().strip() != 'ok':
   188	+        raise RuntimeError('cache drop failed')
   189	+    (bridge/'drop_request').unlink()
   190	+    (bridge/'drop_done').unlink()
   191	+
   192	+
   193	+def prime(paths):
   194	+    """Read every file once so a non-cold test does not depend on what ran before (page cache warm)."""
   195	+    began, n = time.monotonic(), 0
   196	+    for p in paths:
   197	+        with Path(p).open('rb') as f:
   198	+            while chunk := f.read(8 << 20):
   199	+                n += len(chunk)
   200	+    return dict(bytes=n, s=round(time.monotonic()-began, 2))
   201	+
   202	+
   203	+def launch(cmd, logpath, bridge, live):
   204	+    """Spawn the owned server and wait for /health; load_s = spawn to /health ok (server readiness)."""
   205	+    v1.free_ports()
   206	+    with logpath.open('w') as log:
   207	+        began = time.monotonic()
   208	+        live['proc'] = proc = v1.spawn(cmd, log)
   209	+    while live.get('pid') is None:
   210	+        if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic()-began > 10:
   211	+            raise RuntimeError('server crashed or owned child not found')
   212	+        try:
   213	+            live['pid'] = v1.server_pid(proc)
   214	+        except (OSError, RuntimeError):
   215	+            time.sleep(.05)
   216	+    v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=live['pid']))
   217	+    while True:
   218	+        if proc.poll() is not None:
   219	+            raise RuntimeError('server crashed during load')
   220	+        try:
   221	+            if v1.post('/health', timeout=1).get('status') == 'ok':
   222	+                break
   223	+        except (OSError, ValueError):
   224	+            pass
   225	+        v2.telemetry(bridge)
   226	+        if time.monotonic()-began >= LOAD_TIMEOUT_S:
   227	+            raise RuntimeError('server load timeout')
   228	+        time.sleep(.05)
   229	+    return time.monotonic()-began
   230	+
   231	+
   232	+def stop(live, bridge, record):
   233	+    v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=None))
   234	+    proc, pid = live.pop('proc', None), live.pop('pid', None)
   235	+    if proc is not None:
   236	+        began = time.monotonic()
   237	+        record['server_exit'] = v1.own_stop(proc, pid)
   238	+        record['stop_s'] = time.monotonic()-began
   239	+        record['exit_clean'] = record['server_exit'] == 0 and not v1.group_alive(proc.pid)
   240	+
   241	+
   242	+def timed(bridge, pid, logpath, kind, name, endpoint, body, timeout):
   243	+    offset = logpath.stat().st_size
   244	+    f0, t = freq_state(), time.monotonic()
   245	+    data = v2.guarded_post(bridge, pid, endpoint, body, timeout)
   246	+    end, f1 = time.monotonic(), freq_state()
   247	+    with logpath.open() as f:
   248	+        f.seek(offset)
   249	+        delta = f.read()
   250	+    row = v1.request_record(name, data, t, end, delta, 'V2' if kind == 'image' else 'T2')
   251	+    row.update(kind=kind, cache_n=row['timings'].get('cache_n'),
   252	+               prompt_per_second=row['timings'].get('prompt_per_second'), freq=freq_delta(f0, f1))
   253	+    return row
   254	+
   255	+
   256	+def run_test(cfg, out, bridge):
   257	+    record = dict(cfg, phase_start=time.monotonic(), label='NOT VALID', reason='interrupted', requests=[],
   258	+                  command=command(cfg), started_epoch=time.time())
   259	+    path, logpath = out/'result.json', out/'server.log'
   260	+    live = {}
   261	+    warm, seq = payloads(cfg)
   262	+    try:
   263	+        if v1.agent_found(log=True):
   264	+            raise RuntimeError('agent found before launch')
   265	+        if cfg['test'] == 'cold_load':
   266	+            drop_caches(bridge)
   267	+        else:
   268	+            record['primed'] = prime(files_of(cfg))
   269	+        record['start'] = snapshot(bridge)
   270	+        if cfg['test'] in LOAD_TESTS:
   271	+            record['measure_start'] = time.monotonic()
   272	+        record['load_s'] = launch(record['command'], logpath, bridge, live)
   273	+        if cfg['test'] in LOAD_TESTS:
   274	+            record['measure_end'] = time.monotonic()
   275	+            record['end'] = snapshot(bridge)
   276	+        pid = live['pid']
   277	+        v1.check_cores(pid)
   278	+        record['reported'] = verify(cfg)
   279	+        record['libs'] = mapped_libs(pid, BUILDS[cfg['build']])
   280	+        if not record['libs']['ok']:
   281	+            raise RuntimeError(f"server mapped libraries outside its build: {record['libs']['libs']}")
   282	+        record['pss_after_load'] = v2.fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
   283	+        if warm:  # discarded; first_response has none, its single request is the measurement
   284	+            v2.guarded_post(bridge, pid, warm[2], warm[3], REQ_TIMEOUT_S)
   285	+        if seq:
   286	+            record['measure_start'] = start = time.monotonic()
   287	+            record['measure_start_snapshot'] = snapshot(bridge)
   288	+            for kind, name, endpoint, body in seq:
   289	+                v2.telemetry(bridge)
   290	+                v1.check_cores(pid)
   291	+                left = TEST_CAP_S - (time.monotonic()-start)
   292	+                if left <= 0:
   293	+                    raise RuntimeError(f'test cap {TEST_CAP_S} s exceeded')
   294	+                record['requests'].append(timed(bridge, pid, logpath, kind, name, endpoint, body, min(REQ_TIMEOUT_S, left)))
   295	+                v1.write(path, record)
   296	+            record['measure_end'] = time.monotonic()
   297	+            record['end'] = snapshot(bridge)
   298	+        record['freq'] = freq_delta((record.get('measure_start_snapshot') or record['start'])['freq'], record['end']['freq'])
   299	+        record['pss_end'] = v2.fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
   300	+        if live['proc'].poll() is not None:
   301	+            raise RuntimeError('server crashed during the test')
   302	+        v1.check_cores(pid)
   303	+        record['stop_start'] = time.monotonic()  # monitor errors from the server's own exit come after this
   304	+        stop(live, bridge, record)
   305	+        if not record['exit_clean']:
   306	+            raise RuntimeError(f"server cleanup not clean (exit {record['server_exit']})")
   307	+        record.update(label='VALID', reason='completed ' + cfg['test'])
   308	+    except (Exception, SystemExit) as e:
   309	+        record.update(label='NOT VALID', reason=str(e))
   310	+    finally:
   311	+        record.setdefault('stop_start', time.monotonic())
   312	+        stop(live, bridge, record)
   313	+        record['stopped'] = time.monotonic()
   314	+        if logpath.exists():
   315	+            record['markers'] = log_markers(logpath.read_text(errors='replace'))
   316	+        record['samples'] = [s for s in v1.rows(bridge/'samples.jsonl')
   317	+                             if record['phase_start']-1 <= s['t'] <= record['stopped']]
   318	+        record['lmk'] = v1.lmk_request(bridge, record['started_epoch'])
   319	+        record['metrics'] = v1.metrics(record)
   320	+        if record['label'] == 'VALID':
   321	+            bad = [] if record['lmk'].get('ok') else ['LMK query failed']
   322	+            if v2.sample_violations(record['samples']):
   323	+                bad.append('sample violation: ' + ', '.join(v2.sample_violations(record['samples'])))
   324	+            if [e for e in v1.rows(bridge/'errors.jsonl') if record['phase_start'] <= e['t'] <= record['stop_start']]:
   325	+                bad.append('sampling failed')
   326	+            if bad:
   327	+                record.update(label='NOT VALID', reason='; '.join(bad))
   328	+        v1.write(path, record)
   329	+    return record
   330	+
   331	+
   332	+def code_paths():
   333	+    return ['benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py', 'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/oneshot.sh'] + v2.code_paths()
   334	+
   335	+
   336	+def manifest(cfg):
   337	+    warm, seq = payloads(cfg)
   338	+    return dict(config=cfg, command=command(cfg),
   339	+                payload_sha256={n: hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(b, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
   340	+                                for _, n, _, b in ([warm] if warm else []) + seq},
   341	+                code_sha256={p: v1.sha(REPO/p) for p in code_paths()},
   342	+                weight_sha256={str(p): v1.sha(p) for p in WEIGHTS},
   343	+                binary_sha256={str(p): v1.sha(p) for b in BUILDS.values() for p in [v1.BINS[b]] + libs_of(b)},
   344	+                constants=dict(rounds=ROUNDS, first_n=FIRST_N, image_tokens=IMAGE_TOKENS, test_cap_s=TEST_CAP_S),
   345	+                base_commit=subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=REPO, text=True).strip(),
   346	+                git_status=subprocess.check_output(['git', 'status', '--porcelain=v1'], cwd=REPO, text=True))
   347	+
   348	+
   349	+FINGERPRINT = ('config', 'command', 'payload_sha256', 'code_sha256', 'weight_sha256', 'binary_sha256', 'constants')
   350	+
   351	+
   352	+def done_before(spec, runs=HERE/'runs'):
   353	+    """A VALID result for the same test, configuration, code, payloads, weights and binaries already exists."""
   354	+    want = {k: spec[k] for k in FINGERPRINT}
   355	+    for result in sorted(runs.glob('*/result.json')):
   356	+        try:
   357	+            prior = json.loads((result.parent/'run.json').read_text())['manifest']
   358	+            if json.loads(result.read_text()).get('label') == 'VALID' and {k: prior.get(k) for k in FINGERPRINT} == want:
   359	+                return result.parent
   360	+        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError):
   361	+            continue
   362	+    return None
   363	+
   364	+
   365	+def check_env(env=os.environ):
   366	+    """Both servers read LLAMA_ARG_* (batch, ubatch, flash attention, mmap, ...) and OpenMP variables from the
   367	+    environment, which the command line would not show; refuse any so both run on their defaults."""
   368	+    found = sorted(k for k in env if ENV_OVERRIDE.match(k))
   369	+    if found:
   370	+        raise SystemExit('environment overrides server settings; unset: ' + ', '.join(found))
   371	+
   372	+
   373	+def preflight(cfg, repeat=False):
   374	+    """Before launch, native and Debian: ports, agents, files/hashes, no shadowing libs, not already done,
   375	+    plus Debian env/imports or native root/battery/cpuset."""
   376	+    v1.free_ports()
   377	+    matches = v1.agent_matches()
   378	+    if matches:
   379	+        v1.log_agent_matches(matches)
   380	+        raise SystemExit('agent found; close Claude/AGY/Codex (see pid/comm/cmdline above)')
   381	+    missing = [str(p) for p in files_of(cfg) if not p.is_file()]
   382	+    if missing or not libs_of(BUILDS[cfg['build']]):
   383	+        raise SystemExit('missing file(s): ' + ', '.join(missing or ['build shared libraries']))
   384	+    # Both launchers list $PREFIX/lib before their own build dir in RUNPATH: a packaged libllama would shadow both.
   385	+    shadow = sorted(p.name for p in (v1.NATIVE.parent/'usr/lib').glob('lib*') if re.match(r'lib(ggml|llama|mtmd)', p.name))
   386	+    if shadow:
   387	+        raise SystemExit('Termux $PREFIX/lib would shadow the build libraries: ' + ', '.join(shadow))
   388	+    freq_state()
   389	+    if Path('/termux-home').is_dir():  # Debian: the side that builds payloads and runs the servers
   390	+        check_env()
   391	+        needed = ['requests'] + (['PIL'] if cfg['test'] == 'vision' else [])
   392	+        if [m for m in needed if importlib.util.find_spec(m) is None]:
   393	+            raise SystemExit('Debian python3 is missing ' + ', '.join(needed) + ' — run: apt-get install python3-requests python3-pil')
   394	+        spec = manifest(cfg)
   395	+        bad = [p for p, h in WEIGHTS.items() if spec['weight_sha256'][str(p)] != h]
   396	+        if bad:
   397	+            raise SystemExit('SHA-256 mismatch: ' + ', '.join(map(str, bad)))
   398	+        if not repeat and (prior := done_before(spec)):
   399	+            raise SystemExit(f'already measured VALID with identical code and configuration: {prior} (--repeat to redo)')
   400	+    else:
   401	+        if subprocess.run(['su', '-c', 'id'], capture_output=True, timeout=10).returncode != 0:
   402	+            raise SystemExit('no root (su) access; telemetry needs root')
   403	+        cap, status = v1.root(f'cat {v1.BAT}/capacity {v1.BAT}/status', 'preflight_battery').split()[:2]
   404	+        if not (cap.isdigit() and int(cap) >= 50 and status == 'Discharging'):
   405	+            raise SystemExit('battery below 50% or not Discharging; unplug the charger')
   406	+        before = os.sched_getaffinity(0)
   407	+        os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
   408	+        stuck = os.sched_getaffinity(0) == {4, 5, 6, 7}
   409	+        os.sched_setaffinity(0, before)
   410	+        if not stuck:
   411	+            raise SystemExit('Termux cpuset blocks cores 4-7; bring Termux to the foreground, screen on')
   412	+    print('PREFLIGHT OK', flush=True)
   413	+
   414	+
   415	+def med(xs):
   416	+    xs = [x for x in xs if x is not None]
   417	+    return round(statistics.median(xs), 2) if xs else None
   418	+
   419	+
   420	+def summary(folder):
   421	+    """One result, from saved files only; missing fields are None (unavailable)."""
   422	+    d = json.loads((Path(folder)/'result.json').read_text())
   423	+    r, m = d['requests'], d.get('metrics', {})
   424	+    by = lambda k: [x for x in r if x['kind'] == k]
   425	+    dn = sum(x.get('draft_n') or 0 for x in r)
   426	+    start = d.get('measure_start_snapshot') or d.get('start') or {}
   427	+    return dict(
   428	+        folder=str(folder), test=d['test'], build=d['build'], mtp=d['mtp'], q8=d['q8'], label=d['label'],
   429	+        reason=d['reason'], load_s=med([d.get('load_s')]), markers=d.get('markers'),
   430	+        first_ms=med([x['wall_ms'] for x in by('first')]),
   431	+        uncached_pp_tok_s=med([x['prompt_per_second'] for x in by('uncached')]),
   432	+        cached_ms=med([x['prompt_ms'] for x in by('cached')]),
   433	+        gen_tok_s=med([x['predicted_per_second'] for x in by('gen')]),
   434	+        draft_acceptance=round(sum(x.get('draft_n_accepted') or 0 for x in r)/dn, 3) if dn else None,
   435	+        image_wall_ms=med([x['wall_ms'] for x in by('image')]),
   436	+        image_tokens=sorted({x.get('image_tokens') for x in by('image')}, key=str),
   437	+        prompt_n={x['input']: x['prompt_tokens'] for x in r}, gen_tokens={x['input']: x['generated_tokens'] for x in r},
   438	+        checks=dict(uncached_cache_n_zero=all(x['cache_n'] == 0 for x in by('uncached')),
   439	+                    cached_reuse=all((x['cache_n'] or 0) > 0 for x in by('cached')),
   440	+                    replies_nonempty=all((x.get('reply') or '').strip() for x in r),
   441	+                    libs_ok=(d.get('libs') or {}).get('ok')),
   442	+        pss_after_load_mib=(d.get('pss_after_load') or {}).get('pss_kib') and round(d['pss_after_load']['pss_kib']/1024),
   443	+        peak_pss_mib=m.get('peak_pss_kib') and round(m['peak_pss_kib']/1024),
   444	+        min_mem_available_mib=m.get('min_mem_available_kib') and round(m['min_mem_available_kib']/1024),
   445	+        mean_battery_w=(m.get('power') or {}).get('mean_battery_w'),
   446	+        start=dict(skin=start.get('skin'), status=start.get('status'), bat_c=start.get('bat_c'),
   447	+                   capacity=start.get('capacity'), max=start.get('max')),
   448	+        skin_end=(d.get('end') or {}).get('skin'), status_max=m.get('status_max'),
   449	+        caps={k: round(v['percent']) for k, v in m.get('caps', {}).items()},
   450	+        freq=d.get('freq'), lmk=d.get('lmk'), exit_clean=d.get('exit_clean'))
   451	+
   452	+
   453	+def conditions(a, b):
   454	+    """Whether two results ran under matched operating conditions; each criterion reported separately."""
   455	+    sa, sb = a['start'], b['start']
   456	+    known = lambda *xs: all(x is not None for x in xs)
   457	+    c = dict(
   458	+        same_test=a['test'] == b['test'],
   459	+        both_valid=a['label'] == b['label'] == 'VALID',
   460	+        start_skin_within=known(sa['skin'], sb['skin']) and abs(sa['skin']-sb['skin']) <= MATCH_SKIN_C,
   461	+        start_status_normal=sa['status'] == sb['status'] == 0,
   462	+        battery_within=known(sa['capacity'], sb['capacity']) and abs(sa['capacity']-sb['capacity']) <= MATCH_BATTERY_PCT,
   463	+        start_caps_equal=bool(sa['max'] and sb['max']) and all(sa['max'].get(p) == sb['max'].get(p) for p in POLICIES))
   464	+    fa, fb = (x['freq'] or {} for x in (a, b))
   465	+    mhz = {p: [(f.get(p) or {}).get('mean_mhz') for f in (fa, fb)] for p in POLICIES}
   466	+    measured = all(known(*v) and abs(v[0]-v[1]) <= MATCH_FREQ_REL*max(v) for v in mhz.values())
   467	+    return dict(c, matched=all(c.values()), start_skin=[sa['skin'], sb['skin']], measured_mean_mhz=mhz,
   468	+                measured_freq_within=measured,
   469	+                note='matched = start conditions only; measured frequency also reflects each build\'s own load')
   470	+
   471	+
   472	+def report(*folders):
   473	+    lines = [summary(f) for f in folders]
   474	+    for line in lines:
   475	+        print(json.dumps(line), flush=True)
   476	+    if len(lines) == 2:
   477	+        a, b = lines
   478	+        num = lambda x: isinstance(x, (int, float)) and not isinstance(x, bool)
   479	+        ratio = {k: round(b[k]/a[k], 3) for k in a if num(a[k]) and num(b[k]) and a[k]}
   480	+        print(json.dumps(dict(second_over_first=ratio, conditions=conditions(a, b),
   481	+                              prompt_n_identical=a['prompt_n'] == b['prompt_n'],
   482	+                              gen_tokens_identical=a['gen_tokens'] == b['gen_tokens'])), flush=True)
   483	+    return lines
   484	+
   485	+
   486	+def parse(argv):
   487	+    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
   488	+    ap.add_argument('--test', choices=TESTS)
   489	+    ap.add_argument('--build', choices=tuple(BUILDS))
   490	+    ap.add_argument('--mtp', action='store_true', help='load the MTP drafter (implied by --test mtp)')
   491	+    ap.add_argument('--q8', action='store_true', help='load the Q8 projector, new build only (implied by --test vision)')
   492	+    ap.add_argument('--repeat', action='store_true', help='measure again although an identical VALID result exists')
   493	+    ap.add_argument('--dry-run', action='store_true')
   494	+    ap.add_argument('--report', type=Path, nargs='+', help='print one result, or two plus their comparison')
   495	+    ap.add_argument('--bridge', type=Path)
   496	+    ap.add_argument('--preflight', action='store_true', help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
   497	+    ap.add_argument('--monitor', type=Path, help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
   498	+    return ap.parse_args(argv)
   499	+
   500	+
   501	+def main(argv=None):
   502	+    a = parse(argv)
   503	+    if a.monitor:
   504	+        return v1.monitor(a.monitor)
   505	+    if a.report:
   506	+        return report(*a.report)
   507	+    if not (a.test and a.build):
   508	+        raise SystemExit('--test and --build are required')
   509	+    try:
   510	+        cfg = config(a.test, a.build, a.mtp, a.q8)
   511	+    except ValueError as e:
   512	+        raise SystemExit(str(e))
   513	+    if a.preflight:
   514	+        return preflight(cfg, a.repeat)
   515	+    if a.dry_run:
   516	+        warm, seq = payloads(cfg)
   517	+        print(DRY_LABEL)
   518	+        print(json.dumps(dict(config=cfg, command=command(cfg), warmup=warm and warm[1],
   519	+                              sequence=[(k, n, e) for k, n, e, _ in seq]), indent=1))
   520	+        return
   521	+    if a.bridge is None or not Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
   522	+        raise SystemExit('launch only through native vis3/oneshot.sh into Debian')
   523	+    out = bridge = a.bridge.resolve()
   524	+    if not (bridge/'owner_launch').is_file():
   525	+        raise SystemExit('missing owner wrapper receipt')
   526	+    if v1.agent_found(log=True):
   527	+        raise SystemExit('agent found; no server started')
   528	+    check_env()
   529	+    v1.free_ports()
   530	+    v2.telemetry(bridge)
   531	+    os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
   532	+    v1.check_cores(os.getpid())
   533	+    for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
   534	+        signal.signal(sig, v1.interrupted)
   535	+    spec = manifest(cfg)
   536	+    if any(spec['weight_sha256'][str(p)] != h for p, h in WEIGHTS.items()):
   537	+        raise SystemExit('model/drafter/projector SHA-256 mismatch')
   538	+    if not a.repeat and (prior := done_before(spec)):
   539	+        raise SystemExit(f'already measured VALID: {prior} (--repeat to redo)')
   540	+    v1.write(out/'run.json', dict(manifest=spec, repeat=a.repeat,
   541	+             note='One independent measurement; no thermal-equivalence gate. Compare with --report A B, '
   542	+                  'which states whether start conditions matched.'))
   543	+    print('RUN_FOLDER='+str(out), flush=True)
   544	+    result = run_test(cfg, out, bridge)
   545	+    print(f"{cfg['test']} {cfg['build']}{' mtp' if cfg['mtp'] else ''}{' q8' if cfg['q8'] else ''}: "
   546	+          f"{result['label']} — {result['reason']}", flush=True)
   547	+    report(out)
   548	+    if result['label'] != 'VALID':
   549	+        raise SystemExit(1)
   550	+
   551	+
   552	+if __name__ == '__main__':
   553	+    try:
   554	+        main()
   555	+    finally:
   556	+        for proc in v1.OWNED:
   557	+            v1.own_stop(proc)

   583	+@contextlib.contextmanager
   584	+def patched(*triples):
   585	+    saved = [(obj, name, getattr(obj, name)) for obj, name, _ in triples]
   586	+    try:
   587	+        for obj, name, value in triples:
   588	+            setattr(obj, name, value)
   589	+        yield
   590	+    finally:
   591	+        for obj, name, value in saved:
   592	+            setattr(obj, name, value)
   593	+
   594	+
   595	+def deployed_command(setup):
   596	+    """The exact shell command server_manager.start_setup builds; os.system and waits are stubbed."""
   597	+    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO))
   598	+    import server_manager as sm
   599	+    calls = []
   600	+    with patched((sm.os, 'system', calls.append), (sm.time, 'sleep', lambda s: None),
   601	+                 (sm, 'wait_for_server', lambda port=8080, timeout=60: True), (sm, 'mtp_draft_ok', lambda: True)):
   602	+        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
   603	+            sm.start_setup(setup)
   604	+    return shlex.split(calls[-1].replace(' 2>/dev/null &', ''))
   605	+

   607	+def test_config():
   608	+    assert v.config('gen', 'old') == dict(test='gen', build='old', mtp=False, q8=False)
   609	+    assert v.config('mtp', 'new')['mtp'] and v.config('vision', 'new')['q8']
   610	+    assert v.config('prompt', 'new', mtp=True, q8=True) == dict(test='prompt', build='new', mtp=True, q8=True)
   611	+    for bad in (('gen', 'old', True, False), ('vision', 'old', False, False), ('cold_load', 'old', False, True),
   612	+                ('x', 'old', False, False), ('gen', 'b1609', False, False)):
   613	+        try:
   614	+            v.config(*bad)
   615	+        except ValueError:
   616	+            continue
   617	+        raise AssertionError(bad)

   620	+def test_commands_match_deployed_flags():
   621	+    prefix = ['taskset', '-c', '4-7', 'timeout', '--signal=TERM', '--kill-after=3', '900']
   622	+    for cfg, setup in ((v.config('gen', 'old'), 'setup_q4'), (v.config('mtp', 'old'), 'setup_q4_mtp')):
   623	+        cmd = v.command(cfg)
   624	+        want = deployed_command(setup)
   625	+        want[want.index('--port') + 1] = '8091'
   626	+        assert cmd[:7] == prefix and cmd[7:] == want, (cmd[7:], want)
   627	+    old, new = v.command(v.config('gen', 'old')), v.command(v.config('gen', 'new'))
   628	+    assert old[7] == str(v1.BINS['b1609-dotprod']) and new[7] == str(v1.BINS['vis'])
   629	+    assert old[:7] + old[8:] == new[:7] + new[8:], 'only the binary may differ between old and new'
   630	+    for test in v.TESTS[:5]:
   631	+        assert v.command(v.config(test, 'new')) == new, 'every plain test launches the same server'
   632	+    vis = v.command(v.config('vision', 'new'))
   633	+    assert vis == new + ['--mmproj', str(v1.Q8), '--image-min-tokens', '70', '--image-max-tokens', '70']
   634	+    assert v.command(v.config('mtp', 'new')) == new + v1.DRAFT_ARGS

   637	+def test_payloads_per_test():
   638	+    seqs = {t: v.payloads(v.config(t, 'new')) for t in v.TESTS}

   644	+    assert all(b['cache_prompt'] is False for k, _, _, b in seq if k == 'uncached')
   645	+    assert all(b['cache_prompt'] for k, _, _, b in seq if k == 'cached')

   649	+    assert seqs['gen'][1][0][3]['n_predict'] == v2.GEN_N, 'first-response body must not mutate gen[0]'

   652	+    assert v.WARMUP_FRAME not in v.VISION_FRAMES and {e for _, _, e, _ in seq} == {'/v1/chat/completions'}

   690	+def test_verify():
   691	+    def fake(vision, spec, n_ctx=2048):
   692	+        return lambda p: ({'modalities': {'vision': vision}, 'build_info': 'b1'} if p == '/props'
   693	+                          else [{'speculative': spec, 'n_ctx': n_ctx}])
   694	+    with patched((v2, 'get', fake(False, False))):
   695	+        assert v.verify(v.config('gen', 'old'))['speculative'] == [False]
   696	+    with patched((v2, 'get', fake(True, True))):
   697	+        assert v.verify(v.config('prompt', 'new', True, True))
   698	+    for get, cfg in ((fake(False, True), v.config('gen', 'old')), (fake(False, False), v.config('vision', 'new')),
   699	+                     (fake(False, False), v.config('mtp', 'new')), (fake(False, False, 4096), v.config('gen', 'new')),
   700	+                     (fake(None, False), v.config('gen', 'new'))):
   701	+        with patched((v2, 'get', get)):
   702	+            try:
   703	+                v.verify(cfg)
   704	+            except RuntimeError:
   705	+                continue
   706	+            raise AssertionError(cfg)

   721	+def test_prime_reads_every_byte():
   722	+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   723	+        a, b = Path(tmp, 'a'), Path(tmp, 'b')
   724	+        a.write_bytes(b'x' * ((8 << 20) + 5))
   725	+        b.write_bytes(b'y' * 7)
   726	+        assert v.prime([a, b])['bytes'] == (8 << 20) + 12
