   729	+class Proc:
   730	+    pid = 4242
   731	+
   732	+    def poll(self):
   733	+        return None
   734	+
   735	+
   736	+def fake_run(tmp, cfg, fail_verify=False, libs_ok=True):
   737	+    out = Path(tmp)
   738	+    calls = dict(drop=0, prime=0, posts=[], stops=[])
   739	+    tele = dict(skin=30., status=0, bat_c=28., capacity=70, voltage_now_uV=4e6, mem_available_kib=1,
   740	+                max=dict(policy4=2348000, policy6=2850000), t=1.)
   741	+
   742	+    def launch(cmd, logpath, bridge, live):
   743	+        live.update(proc=Proc(), pid=99)
   744	+        logpath.write_text(T1_LOG.read_text(errors='replace'))
   745	+        return 1.5
   746	+
   747	+    def timed(bridge, pid, logpath, kind, name, endpoint, body, timeout):
   748	+        return dict(kind=kind, input=name, wall_ms=100., prompt_ms=50., prompt_tokens=10, generated_tokens=5,
   749	+                    predicted_per_second=10., prompt_per_second=200., cache_n=0 if kind != 'cached' else 9,
   750	+                    reply='ok', draft_n=None, draft_n_accepted=None, freq=None)
   751	+
   752	+    def verify(c):
   753	+        if fail_verify:
   754	+            raise RuntimeError('configuration mismatch')
   755	+        return dict(vision=c['q8'], speculative=[c['mtp']], n_ctx=[2048], build_info='b')
   756	+    freq = dict(policy4={2348000: 10}, policy6={2850000: 10})
   757	+    with patched((v, 'launch', launch), (v, 'timed', timed), (v, 'verify', verify),
   758	+                 (v, 'mapped_libs', lambda pid, b: dict(libs=['/elsewhere/libllama.so'], ok=libs_ok)),
   759	+                 (v, 'drop_caches', lambda b: calls.__setitem__('drop', calls['drop'] + 1)),
   760	+                 (v, 'prime', lambda paths: calls.__setitem__('prime', calls['prime'] + 1) or dict(bytes=1, s=0)),
   761	+                 (v, 'freq_state', lambda root=None: freq),
   762	+                 (v2, 'telemetry', lambda b: tele), (v2, 'fresh_pss', lambda b, p: dict(pss_kib=2048)),
   763	+                 (v2, 'guarded_post', lambda b, p, e, body, t: calls['posts'].append(e) or {}),
   764	+                 (v1, 'check_cores', lambda pid: None), (v1, 'agent_found', lambda log=False: False),
   765	+                 (v1, 'own_stop', lambda proc, pid=None: calls['stops'].append(pid) or 0),
   766	+                 (v1, 'group_alive', lambda g: False), (v1, 'lmk_request', lambda b, e: dict(ok=True)),
   767	+                 (v1, 'metrics', lambda r: {})):
   768	+        rec = v.run_test(cfg, out, out)
   769	+        saved = json.loads((out/'result.json').read_text())
   770	+        target = json.loads((out/'target.json').read_text())
   771	+    return rec, calls, saved, target
   772	+

   804	+def test_done_before():
   805	+    spec = dict(config=dict(test='gen'), command=['x'], payload_sha256={}, code_sha256={'a': 1}, weight_sha256={},
   806	+                binary_sha256={}, constants={}, git_status='?? other')
   807	+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   808	+        runs = Path(tmp)
   809	+        assert v.done_before(spec, runs) is None
   810	+        for name, label, man in (('r1', 'NOT VALID', spec), ('r2', 'VALID', dict(spec, code_sha256={'a': 2})),
   811	+                                 ('old_format', 'VALID', None)):
   812	+            (runs/name).mkdir()
   813	+            if man:
   814	+                (runs/name/'run.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(manifest=man)))
   815	+                (runs/name/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(label=label)))
   816	+            else:
   817	+                (runs/name/'00_O_x.json').write_text('{}')
   818	+        assert v.done_before(spec, runs) is None, 'invalid, different code and old-format folders do not count'
   819	+        (runs/'r3').mkdir()
   820	+        (runs/'r3/run.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(manifest=dict(spec, git_status=''))))
   821	+        (runs/'r3/result.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(label='VALID')))
   822	+        assert v.done_before(spec, runs) == runs/'r3', 'git status is not part of the fingerprint'
   823	+

   825	+def test_preflight_refusals():
   826	+    cfg = v.config('gen', 'new')
   827	+    spec = dict(weight_sha256={str(p): h for p, h in v.WEIGHTS.items()})
   828	+    real = v.importlib.util.find_spec
   829	+    base = ((v1, 'free_ports', lambda: None), (v1, 'agent_matches', lambda: []), (v, 'check_env', lambda: None),
   830	+            (v, 'manifest', lambda c: spec))
   831	+    with patched(*base, (v.importlib.util, 'find_spec', lambda m: None if m == 'requests' else real(m))):
   832	+        try:
   833	+            with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()) as out:
   834	+                v.preflight(cfg)
   835	+        except SystemExit as e:
   836	+            assert 'requests' in str(e) and 'PREFLIGHT OK' not in out.getvalue()
   837	+        else:
   838	+            raise AssertionError('preflight must refuse without requests')
   839	+    with patched(*base, (v, 'done_before', lambda s: Path('runs/prior'))):
   840	+        try:
   841	+            v.preflight(cfg)
   842	+        except SystemExit as e:
   843	+            assert 'already measured' in str(e)
   844	+        else:
   845	+            raise AssertionError('preflight must refuse a repeated VALID test')
   846	+        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()) as out:
   847	+            v.preflight(cfg, repeat=True)
   848	+        assert 'PREFLIGHT OK' in out.getvalue()
   849	+
   850	+
   851	+def result(build, skin, gen_tps, capacity=70, caps=2850000, mhz=2000, tokens=5):
   852	+    req = dict(kind='gen', input='A5', wall_ms=100., prompt_ms=50., prompt_tokens=10, generated_tokens=tokens,
   853	+               predicted_per_second=gen_tps, prompt_per_second=100., cache_n=0, reply='ok', draft_n=None,
   854	+               draft_n_accepted=None)
   855	+    start = dict(skin=skin, status=0, bat_c=28., capacity=capacity, max=dict(policy0=1, policy4=2348000, policy6=caps))
   856	+    return dict(test='gen', build=build, mtp=False, q8=False, label='VALID', reason='', load_s=4., requests=[req],
   857	+                start=start, measure_start_snapshot=start, end=dict(skin=skin + 3), libs=dict(ok=True),
   858	+                pss_after_load=dict(pss_kib=3 << 20), exit_clean=True,
   859	+                freq={p: dict(ticks=10, mean_mhz=mhz, top_share=1.) for p in v.POLICIES},
   860	+                metrics=dict(peak_pss_kib=4 << 20, min_mem_available_kib=2 << 20, power=dict(mean_battery_w=4.)))
   861	+
   862	+
   863	+def report_of(a, b):
   864	+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   865	+        for name, rec in (('a', a), ('b', b)):
   866	+            Path(tmp, name).mkdir()
   867	+            Path(tmp, name, 'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(rec))
   868	+        out = io.StringIO()
   869	+        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
   870	+            lines = v.report(Path(tmp, 'a'), Path(tmp, 'b'))
   871	+    return lines, json.loads(out.getvalue().splitlines()[-1])
   872	+
   873	+
   874	+def test_report_states_whether_conditions_match():
   875	+    lines, last = report_of(result('old', 30., 12.), result('new', 30.6, 9.))
   876	+    assert lines[0]['gen_tok_s'] == 12 and lines[1]['pss_after_load_mib'] == 3072 and lines[0]['skin_end'] == 33.
   877	+    assert last['second_over_first']['gen_tok_s'] == .75 and last['prompt_n_identical'] and last['gen_tokens_identical']
   878	+    c = last['conditions']
   879	+    assert c['matched'] and c['measured_freq_within'] and c['start_skin'] == [30., 30.6]
   880	+    for b, failed in ((result('new', 31.5, 9.), 'start_skin_within'), (result('new', 30., 9., capacity=55), 'battery_within'),
   881	+                      (result('new', 30., 9., caps=1582000), 'start_caps_equal')):
   882	+        _, last = report_of(result('old', 30., 12.), b)
   883	+        assert not last['conditions']['matched'] and not last['conditions'][failed], failed
   884	+    _, last = report_of(result('old', 30., 12.), result('new', 30., 9., mhz=1800))
   885	+    assert last['conditions']['matched'] and not last['conditions']['measured_freq_within']
   886	+    other = result('new', 30., 9.)
   887	+    other['measure_start_snapshot'] = dict(other['start'], max=dict(other['start']['max'], policy0=5))
   888	+    assert report_of(result('old', 30., 12.), other)[1]['conditions']['start_caps_equal'], 'little cores ignored'
   889	+
   890	+
   891	+def test_dry_run():
   892	+    for test in v.TESTS:
   893	+        out = io.StringIO()
   894	+        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
   895	+            v.main(['--dry-run', '--test', test, '--build', 'new'])
   896	+        text = out.getvalue()
   897	+        assert text.startswith(v.DRY_LABEL)
   898	+        assert json.loads(text.split('\n', 1)[1])['config']['test'] == test
   899	+    try:
   900	+        v.main(['--dry-run', '--test', 'vision', '--build', 'old'])
   901	+    except SystemExit as e:
   902	+        assert 'new build only' in str(e)
   903	+    else:
   904	+        raise AssertionError('vision on old must be refused')
   905	+

   912	+    assert 'NAME=$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ)$SUFFIX' in text

   916	+    for name, fn in list(globals().items()):

   918	+            fn()
   919	+            print('ok', name)

   926	+#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
   927	+# Marked adaptation of vis2/oneshot.sh (itself adapted from vis1): vis3 paths and runner; also refuses while a VIS-2 lock exists.
   928	+# VIS-3B: one test per launch (args go to both preflights), folder named after the test, no 5-minute idle.
   929	+set -u
   930	+H=/data/data/com.termux/files/home
   931	+R=$H/robot/benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3
   932	+PY=/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python3
   933	+if [ -d /termux-home ] || [ "${PREFIX:-}" != /data/data/com.termux/files/usr ]; then
   934	+  echo 'Run from native Termux, not Debian.'; exit 1
   935	+fi
   936	+export PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1
   937	+PREFLIGHT=0
   938	+SUFFIX=
   939	+PREV=
   940	+for ARG in "$@"; do
   941	+  [ "$ARG" = --preflight ] && PREFLIGHT=1
   942	+  case "$PREV" in --test|--build) SUFFIX="${SUFFIX}_$ARG" ;; esac
   943	+  case "$ARG" in --mtp|--q8|--repeat) SUFFIX="${SUFFIX}_${ARG#--}" ;; esac
   944	+  PREV=$ARG
   945	+done
   946	+# Preflight (argparse choices) validates --test/--build before SUFFIX names a folder.
   947	+PARGS=()
   948	+for ARG in "$@"; do [ "$ARG" = --preflight ] || PARGS+=("$ARG"); done
   949	+for L in "$H/robot/benchmark/gemma_vision/vis1/runs/.lock" "$H/robot/benchmark/gemma_vision/vis2/runs/.lock" "$R/runs/.lock"; do
   950	+  [ -e "$L" ] && { echo "Existing benchmark lock: $L; inspect before removing it."; exit 1; }
   951	+done
   952	+"$PY" -B "$R/vis3_run.py" --preflight "${PARGS[@]}" || exit 1
   953	+proot-distro login debian --bind "$H:/termux-home" -- /bin/bash -c \
   954	+  'cd /termux-home/robot && PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py --preflight "$@"' \
   955	+  vis3 "${PARGS[@]}" || exit 1
   956	+su -c id >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'No Magisk root'; exit 1; }
   957	+if [ "$PREFLIGHT" = 1 ]; then echo 'VIS-3 READY (native + Debian preflight passed; nothing started).'; exit 0; fi
   958	+mkdir -p "$R/runs"
   959	+LOCK=$R/runs/.lock
   960	+mkdir "$LOCK" 2>/dev/null || { echo "Existing launch lock: $LOCK; inspect before removing it."; exit 1; }

   968	+OLD_TIMEOUT=
   969	+RUNNER=
   970	+MONITOR=
   971	+CHILD=
   972	+cleanup() {
   973	+  touch "$B/stop"
   974	+  [ -z "$MONITOR" ] || wait "$MONITOR" 2>/dev/null
   975	+  if [[ $OLD_TIMEOUT =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
   976	+    su -c "settings put system screen_off_timeout $OLD_TIMEOUT </dev/null >$S 2>&1"
   977	+    su -c "settings get system screen_off_timeout </dev/null >$S 2>&1"
   978	+    BACK=$(su -c "cat $S" | tr -d '[:space:]')
   979	+    [ "$BACK" = "$OLD_TIMEOUT" ] || echo "WARNING: restore screen timeout manually to $OLD_TIMEOUT"
   980	+  fi
   981	+  termux-wake-unlock
   982	+  rmdir "$LOCK" 2>/dev/null || { rm -f "$LOCK/pid"; rmdir "$LOCK"; }
   983	+}
   984	+trap cleanup EXIT
   985	+on_signal() {
   986	+  if [ -n "$RUNNER" ]; then
   987	+    # proot may not forward signals: the runner also watches bridge/abort every telemetry check.
   988	+    touch "$B/abort"
   989	+  else
   990	+    exit 130
   991	+  fi
   992	+}
   993	+trap on_signal INT TERM HUP
   994	+check_battery() {
   995	+  readarray -t BATTERY < <(su -c 'cat /sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity /sys/class/power_supply/battery/status')
   996	+  [[ ${BATTERY[0]:-} =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] && [ "${BATTERY[0]}" -ge 50 ] &&
   997	+    [ "${BATTERY[1]:-}" = Discharging ] || { echo 'Need >=50% battery, charger unplugged.'; exit 1; }
   998	+}

  1016	+      echo "$value" > "$B/drop_done.tmp"
  1017	+      mv "$B/drop_done.tmp" "$B/drop_done"
  1018	+    fi
  1019	+    sleep .2
  1020	+  done ) &
  1021	+CHILD=$!

  1023	+  'cd /termux-home/robot && PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 python3 -B benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py "$@"' \

  1027	+wait "$RUNNER"; RC=$?
  1028	+touch "$B/stop"
  1029	+wait "$CHILD"
  1030	+echo "VIS-3 exited $RC. Folder: $B" | tee -a "$CONSOLE"

  1038	+# VIS-3B: b1609-dotprod (old) vs b11716 (new), one independent test per launch, motors off
  1039	+
  1040	+Preparation only: no measured results yet. The owner runs it from native Termux with no agent resident. `vis3_run.py` imports
  1041	+`vis2/vis2_run.py` unchanged (and through it `vis1/vis1_run.py`). It reuses their telemetry bridge, battery, thermal,
  1042	+abort, agent and affinity stops, cache-drop handshake, owned-process stop, LMK query, PSS and power.
  1043	+
  1044	+VIS-3B (owner, 2026-10-22) replaced the multi-phase O,N,N,O design. Each launch runs one test in one configuration,
  1045	+saves it in its own folder and exits; nothing runs automatically after it. The 32 °C gate and the cooling wait are gone
  1046	+(the timed run `runs/20261022T153641Z` missed that gate at idle skin 32.95 °C). The genuine stops remain:
  1047	+battery ≥ 50 % Discharging, Android CRITICAL, battery ≥ 45 °C, owner abort, agent, cores 4–7, LMK, clean exit.
  1048	+Earlier folders in `runs/` are preserved as they are.
  1049	+
  1050	+## Why (diagnosis, read-only)
  1051	+
  1052	+The builds are equivalent: same Clang 21.1.8, `-O3`, `-march=armv8.2-a+dotprod+fp16`, OpenMP, REPACK, LLAMAFILE,
  1053	+shared libraries, no KleidiAI. Both use `sdot` (1045 vs 1117) and 456 FP16 `fmla .8h`. The effective runtime defaults
  1054	+also match: mmap (OLD default `mmap`, NEW `auto` → mmap on CPU), flash attention `auto` → enabled, batch 2048 / ubatch
  1055	+512, `--fit on`, `--swa-full` and `--cache-ram 0` honoured, and the MTP draft inherits the target's threads. The earlier
  1056	+OLD/NEW figures were taken at start skins between 24 and 39 °C with capped CPUs, so they do not isolate the build. Full
  1057	+evidence is in `reports/VIS3_CODER_REPORT.md`.
  1058	+
  1059	+## Tests
  1060	+
  1061	+| `--test` | Measures | Before the measurement |
  1062	+|---|---|---|
  1063	+| `cold_load` | spawn → `/health` ok, log-phase timestamps | page-cache drop (native handshake) |
  1064	+| `warm_load` | spawn → `/health` ok | every file the server reads, read once (`prime`) |
  1065	+| `first_response` | wall time of the first request (gen[0] prompt, 16 tokens) | prime, load, no warm-up |
  1066	+| `prompt` | 4 × (U ≈1005-token uncached, K cached prefix), 8 tokens each | prime, load, 1 discarded request |
  1067	+| `gen` | the 6 VIS-2 prompts, greedy, ≤ 128 tokens, no drafter | prime, load, 1 discarded request |
  1068	+| `mtp` | as `gen`, with the drafter (`--spec-draft-n-max 3`); draft acceptance | as `gen` |
  1069	+| `vision` | 4 VIS-1 frames through the Q8 projector, 70 image tokens (new build only) | prime, load, 1 discarded image |
  1070	+
  1071	+`--build old|new` is required. `--mtp` adds the drafter to any test except `gen` (use `--test mtp`). `--q8` adds the
  1072	+projector on the new build (implied by `vision`). The command is exactly `server_manager.start_setup('setup_q4')` (ctx
  1073	+2048, 4/4 threads, parallel 1, `--swa-full --cache-ram 0`), plus `taskset -c 4-7`, `timeout 900` and port 8091. Old and
  1074	+new differ only in the binary path (test-checked against `server_manager.py`).
  1075	+
  1076	+Each test checks:
  1077	+- `/props` and `/slots` (projector, drafter, n_ctx 2048);
  1078	+- `/proc/<pid>/maps` (only the build's own libraries);
  1079	+- hidden-setting environment variables (`LLAMA_*`, `GGML_*`, `OMP_*`, `KMP_*`, `LD_LIBRARY_PATH`, `LD_PRELOAD`), which
  1080	+  are refused.
  1081	+
  1082	+Each test records:
  1083	+- operating conditions at the start and end of the measurement: skin, Android status, battery °C, %, V and W, CPU caps,
  1084	+  MemAvailable, zone temperatures;
  1085	+- mean CPU frequency and time at top frequency (policy4/6, from cpufreq `time_in_state`) over the measurement and per
  1086	+  request;
  1087	+- PSS after load and at the end, peak PSS, minimum MemAvailable;
  1088	+- mean battery W, caps %, LMK, exit code and stop time.
  1089	+
  1090	+A test that already has a VALID result with identical configuration, code, payloads, weights and binaries is refused
  1091	+by preflight (`--repeat` to measure it again).
  1092	+
  1093	+## Owner commands (native Termux, no agent running, battery ≥ 50 % and unplugged, screen on, Termux in front)
  1094	+
  1095	+```bash
  1096	+O=~/robot/benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3
  1097	+bash $O/oneshot.sh --preflight --test gen --build old       # readiness for that test, starts nothing
  1098	+bash $O/oneshot.sh --test cold_load --build old
  1099	+bash $O/oneshot.sh --test cold_load --build new
  1100	+bash $O/oneshot.sh --test gen --build old
  1101	+bash $O/oneshot.sh --test gen --build new
  1102	+# likewise: warm_load, first_response, prompt, mtp (old/new), vision (new), --mtp / --q8 variants
  1103	+python3 -B $O/vis3_run.py --report $O/runs/<old folder> $O/runs/<new folder>
  1104	+```
  1105	+
  1106	+Expected time per launch: about 10 s for the monitor to start, then load 5–10 s and priming a few seconds.
  1107	+Measurement: load tests about 10 s, `first_response` about 3 s, `prompt` about 3 min, `gen`/`mtp` 1–2 min, `vision`
  1108	+about 1 min. For comparable pairs, run old and new back to back and alternate their order when repeating.
  1109	+
  1110	+Folders: `vis3/runs/<UTC>_<test>_<build>[_mtp][_q8][_repeat]/` with `run.json` (configuration, command, payload, code,
  1111	+weight and binary/library hashes, base commit, git status), `result.json`, `server.log`, `samples.jsonl`, `console.log`.
  1112	+
  1113	+`--report A B` prints both results, B/A ratios and `conditions`, stating whether the two ran under matched conditions:
  1114	+- same test, both VALID;
  1115	+- start skin within 1.0 °C, both at Android status 0;
  1116	+- battery within 10 %;
  1117	+- equal policy4/6 caps at the start.
  1118	+
  1119	+It also reports separately whether the measured mean frequencies are within 5 % (informational: they also reflect each
  1120	+build's own load), and whether prompt token counts and generated lengths are identical. Unmatched pairs are labelled as
  1121	+such, not discarded. Short tests do not establish thermal equilibrium (#93). These are Termux prototype results, not
  1122	+the Android app.

codex
The strongest issues are in comparison and repeat detection: the report can call different configurations matched, and changing an unused build changes the fingerprint of a completed test. I also checked the shutdown race further: because there is now only one server phase and shutdown errors fall outside the validity window, the removed detachment wait is not itself a blocking finding.

codex
Found **two P2 issues and one P3 issue**. No P0/P1 safety regression identified.

1. **P2 — Cross-run reports no longer verify comparable configurations or provenance.**

   `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py:416`:
   ```python
       d = json.loads((Path(folder)/'result.json').read_text())
   ```

   `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py:452`:
   ```python
           same_test=a['test'] == b['test'],
   ```

   `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py:461`:
   ```python
       return dict(c, matched=all(c.values()), start_skin=[sa['skin'], sb['skin']], measured_mean_mhz=mhz,
   ```

   The report never reads the saved manifests. Two `prompt` runs can therefore produce ratios and `matched: true` when one includes the drafter and the other does not, or when their payloads, context settings, weights, or harness code differ. Identical token counts do not establish identical prompts.

   Restore a separate, explicit comparability assessment using the manifests, allowing the intended configuration differences. Keep operating-condition matching separate. Add tests covering different MTP/projector settings and different payload/code hashes.

2. **P2 — Unused artifacts prevent independent tests and defeat repeat detection.**

   `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py:336`:
   ```python
                   weight_sha256={str(p): v1.sha(p) for p in WEIGHTS},
   ```

   `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py:337`:
   ```python
                   binary_sha256={str(p): v1.sha(p) for b in BUILDS.values() for p in [v1.BINS[b]] + libs_of(b)},
   ```

   Every test hashes all three weights and both builds, including artifacts it never uses. Consequently:

   - An OLD text-only test fails during Debian preflight if the unused NEW executable or Q8 projector is absent.
   - Rebuilding only NEW changes the fingerprint of an already completed OLD test, permitting that unchanged OLD measurement to run again without `--repeat`.

   Hash and validate the artifacts used by the selected configuration for execution and repeat detection. An optional broader inventory should not determine completion identity. Add tests for missing unused artifacts and changes to the unselected build.

3. **P3 — Folder names depend on argument spelling and order.**

   `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/oneshot.sh:17`:
   ```bash
     case "$PREV" in --test|--build) SUFFIX="${SUFFIX}_$ARG" ;; esac
   ```

   `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/oneshot.sh:36`:
   ```bash
   NAME=$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ)$SUFFIX
   ```

   Argparse accepts `--test=gen --build=new`, but the wrapper produces a timestamp-only folder. Reversing the ordinary arguments produces `_new_gen`; repeated options can put superseded values into the name. This undermines the documented configuration-labelled archive.

   Derive the suffix from the validated, normalized configuration. Test both argument forms and reversed ordering; the current wrapper test only checks source strings.

The measurement selection otherwise follows the requested design: load tests send no inference requests; cold loading uses the cache-drop handshake; other tests prime their selected files; first-response has no harness warm-up; generation and MTP use the same measured payloads. Frequency counters bracket individual requests, and reused power/capping calculations use the saved measurement interval. First-response is explicitly full-response latency for up to 16 generated tokens, not time to first token.

The candidate retains the reused battery, thermal, telemetry freshness, abort, agent, affinity, owned-process cleanup, and memory/LMK observation paths. Removing sampler detachment does not recreate the previous intermediate cold-to-warm failure: this runner has one server phase, with shutdown outside its sampling-error validity window. Historical run folders are excluded from completion detection by their old record layout.
